import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import activities from '../activities.json';

describe('activities.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  it('każda aktywność ma wymagane pola', () => {
    for (const a of activities) {
      expect(a.id, `Aktywność bez id`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(a.label, `${a.id}: brak label`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(a.description, `${a.id}: brak description`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(typeof a.weight, `${a.id}: weight musi być liczbą`).toBe('number');
      expect(a.conditions, `${a.id}: brak conditions`).toBeDefined();
      expect(a.effects, `${a.id}: brak effects`).toBeDefined();
    }
  });

  it('id aktywności są unikalne', () => {
    const ids = activities.map((a: any) => a.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });
});
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\data\__tests__\competition.test.ts ===
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from './initialState';
import {
  generateCompetitors,
  tickCompetitors,
  computeRanking,
  interactWithCompetitor,
  ensureCompetitors,
  getCategoryId,
  SIZE_ORDER,
  INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN,
} from './competition';
import type { GameState } from './types';
import competitionData from '../data/competition.json';
import branchesData from '../data/branches.json';

const SIZES = new Set<string>(SIZE_ORDER);

function newState(seed = 'test-seed', activity = 'handel_odziez'): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed,
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity,
    companyName: 'Testex',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
  });
}

/** Symuluje upływ czasu: turn++, kwartały, lata — bez reszty silnika. */
function advance(s: GameState, ticks: number) {
  for (let i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
    s.turn += 1;
    const total = s.year * 4 + (s.quarter - 1) + 1;
    s.year = Math.floor(total / 4);
    s.quarter = ((total % 4) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
    tickCompetitors(s, 0);
  }
}

describe('data/competition.json', () => {
  const categoryIds = new Set((branchesData as { id: string }[]).map((c) => c.id));
  const templates = competitionData as any[];

  it('ma unikalne id i wymagane pola', () => {
    const ids = templates.map((t) => t.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const t of templates) {
      expect(typeof t.name).toBe('string');
      expect(typeof t.description).toBe('string');
      expect(t.baseReputation).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(t.baseReputation).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
    }
  });

  it('opcjonalne pola, jeśli występują, mają poprawne wartości', () => {
    for (const t of templates) {
      if (t.categories) for (const c of t.categories) expect(c === '*' || categoryIds.has(c)).toBe(true);
      if (t.baseSize) expect(SIZES.has(t.baseSize)).toBe(true);
      for (const k of ['baseCondition', 'baseOpinion']) {
        if (t[k] !== undefined) {
          expect(t[k]).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
          expect(t[k]).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
        }
      }
      if (t.baseValue !== undefined) expect(t.baseValue).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });
});

describe('generateCompetitors', () => {
  it('jest deterministyczne', () => {
    expect(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989)).toEqual(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989));
  });

  it('zwraca unikalne firmy', () => {
    const list = generateCompetitors('produkcja', 1989);
    expect(new Set(list.map((c) => c.id)).size).toBe(list.length);
  });
});

describe('tickCompetitors', () => {
  it('jest deterministyczne dla tego samego seeda', () => {
    const a = newState('same');
    const b = newState('same');
    advance(a, 40);
    advance(b, 40);
    expect(a.competitors).toEqual(b.competitors);
  });

  it('utrzymuje statystyki w granicach przez 160 tur', () => {
    const s = newState('bounds');
    advance(s, 160);
    for (const c of s.competitors) {
      for (const v of [c.reputation, c.condition, c.opinionOfPlayer]) {
        expect(v).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(v).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
      }
      expect(c.value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1000);
      expect(SIZES.has(c.size)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('regeneruje konkurencję po zmianie kategorii działalności', () => {
    const s = newState('switch');
    s.company.activity = 'gast_kebab';
    advance(s, 1);
    expect(s.competitionCategory).toBe('gastronomia');
  });
});

describe('computeRanking', () => {
  it('sortuje malejąco, numeruje od 1 i zawiera gracza dokładnie raz', () => {
    const s = newState('rank');
    advance(s, 20);
    const rows = computeRanking(s);
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.isPlayer)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(s.competitors.length + 1);
    rows.forEach((r, i) => expect(r.rank).toBe(i + 1));
    for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) expect(rows[i - 1].score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(rows[i].score);
  });
});

describe('interactWithCompetitor', () => {
  it('zużywa punkt interakcji i nie mutuje starego stanu', () => {
    const s = newState('interact');
    const id = s.competitors[0].id;
    const next = interactWithCompetitor(s, id, 'positive');
    expect(next.interactionPoints).toBe(s.interactionPoints - 1);
    expect(next).not.toBe(s);
    expect(s.interactionPoints).toBe(INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN);
  });

  it('bez punktów albo dla nieznanego id zwraca ten sam obiekt', () => {
    const s = newState('nopoints');
    s.interactionPoints = 0;
    expect(interactWithCompetitor(s, s.competitors[0].id, 'positive')).toBe(s);
    const s2 = newState('unknown');
    expect(interactWithCompetitor(s2, 'nie_ma_takiego', 'negative')).toBe(s2);
  });
});

describe('ensureCompetitors (migracja zapisu)', () => {
  it('uzupełnia brakujące pola w starym zapisie', () => {
    const s = newState('migrate') as any;
    delete s.competitors;
    delete s.competitionCategory;
    const migrated = ensureCompetitors(s);
    expect(Array.isArray(migrated.competitors)).toBe(true);
    expect(migrated.competitionCategory).toBe(getCategoryId('handel_odziez'));
  });

  it('nie rusza stanu, który już ma konkurentów', () => {
    const s = newState('nochange');
    expect(ensureCompetitors(s)).toBe(s);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import events from '../events.json';
import activities from '../activities.json';
import parties from '../parties.json';
import type { GameEvent, Activity, Party } from '../../engine/types';

// --- 1. WALIDACJA EVENTÓW ---
describe('events.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  const eventList = events as GameEvent[];

  it('każdy event ma wymagane pola', () => {
    for (const ev of eventList) {
      expect(ev.id, `Event bez id`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(ev.title, `${ev.id}: brak title`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(ev.description, `${ev.id}: brak description`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(typeof ev.weight, `${ev.id}: weight musi być liczbą`).toBe('number');
      expect(ev.conditions, `${ev.id}: brak conditions`).toBeDefined();
      expect(ev.choices, `${ev.id}: brak choices`).toBeDefined();
    }
  });

  it('każdy event ma co najmniej jedną opcję', () => {
    for (const ev of eventList) {
      expect(ev.choices.length, `${ev.id}: 0 opcji`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it('każda opcja ma label i (effects lub gamble lub outcomes)', () => {
    for (const ev of eventList) {
      for (const c of ev.choices) {
        expect(c.label, `${ev.id}/${c.id}: brak label`).toBeTruthy();
        const hasEffect = c.effects || c.gamble || c.outcomes;
        expect(hasEffect, `${ev.id}/${c.id}: brak efektu`).toBeTruthy();
      }
    }
  });
});

// --- 2. WALIDACJA AKTYWNOŚCI (Daily Actions) ---
describe('activities.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  const activityList = activities as Activity[];

  it('każda aktywność ma wymagane pola', () => {
    for (const a of activityList) {
      expect(a.id, `Aktywność bez id`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(a.label, `${a.id}: brak label`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(a.description, `${a.id}: brak description`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(typeof a.weight, `${a.id}: weight musi być liczbą`).toBe('number');
    }
  });

  it('id aktywności są unikalne', () => {
    const ids = activityList.map(a => a.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });
});

// --- 3. WALIDACJA PARTII ---
describe('parties.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  const partyList = parties as Party[];

  it('każda partia ma wymagane pola', () => {
    for (const p of partyList) {
      expect(p.id).toBeTruthy();
      expect(p.name, `${p.id}: brak name`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(p.ideology, `${p.id}: brak ideology`).toBeTruthy();
      
      expect(p.sizeSchedule, `${p.id}: brak sizeSchedule`).toBeDefined();
      expect(Array.isArray(p.sizeSchedule), `${p.id}: sizeSchedule nie jest tablicą`).toBe(true);
      
      expect(typeof p.activeFrom).toBe('number');
      expect(typeof p.activeTo).toBe('number');
    }
  });

  it('sizeSchedule ma poprawne przedziały czasowe', () => {
    for (const p of partyList) {
      for (const sched of p.sizeSchedule) {
        expect(sched.from, `${p.id}: sizeSchedule from > to`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(sched.to);
        expect(sched.size, `${p.id}: brak size w sizeSchedule`).toBeTruthy();
      }
    }
  });
});
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\engine\__tests__\actions.test.ts ===
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from '../initialState';
import {
  applyHire, applyLoan, applyChangeActivity, applyJoinParty,
  applyLeaveParty, applyEmergencyLoan,
} from '../actions';
import { WORKER_CONFIG, MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE } from '../constants';
import type { GameState } from '../types';

function makeState(): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed: 'test',
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity: 'handel',
    companyName: 'Test',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
  });
}

describe('applyHire', () => {
  it('zwiększa liczbę pracowników', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = applyHire(state, 'zlecenie', 3);
    expect(result.company.employees.zlecenie).toBe(3);
  });

  it('nie pozwala zejść poniżej zera', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = applyHire(state, 'zlecenie', -5);
    expect(result.company.employees.zlecenie).toBe(0);
  });

  it('przy zwolnieniu UOP zabiera odprawę z kasy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 2;
    const cashBefore = state.company.finances.stanKonta;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'uop', -2);

    const expectedSeverance = 2 * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.severance;
    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(cashBefore - expectedSeverance);
  });

  it('nie zwalnia UOP gdy nie stać na odprawę', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 5;
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = 1000;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'uop', -5);

    expect(result.company.employees.uop).toBe(5);
    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(1000);
  });

  it('zwolnienie bez umowy obniża opinię pracowników', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.bezUmowy = 3;
    const repBefore = state.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'bezUmowy', -2);

    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy).toBeLessThan(repBefore);
  });

  it('zwolnienie UOP nie obniża opinii pracowników (bo jest odprawa)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 2;
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = 100_000;
    const repBefore = state.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'uop', -1);

    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy).toBe(repBefore);
  });
});

describe('applyLoan', () => {
  it('zwiększa stan konta o kwotę kredytu', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const before = state.company.finances.stanKonta;

    const result = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);

    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(before + 20_000);
  });

  it('dodaje kredyt do listy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);
    expect(result.company.loans).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('kredyt z szarej strefy ma wyższe odsetki niż bankowy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const bank = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);
    const strefa = applyLoan(state, 'szara_strefa', 20_000);

    expect(strefa.company.loans[0].interestRate).toBeGreaterThan(
      bank.company.loans[0].interestRate
    );
  });
});

describe('applyChangeActivity', () => {
  it('zmienia działalność firmy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.turn = 10; // minęło dość tur
    const result = applyChangeActivity(state, 'it');
    expect(result.company.activity).toBe('it');
  });

  it('resetuje opinię mafii do baseline nowej branży', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.turn = 10;
    const result = applyChangeActivity(state, 'windykacja');
    
    // Zamiast starej stałej, sprawdzamy, czy opinia mafii zmieniła się na wartość z branches.json
    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaMafia).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaMafia).toBeLessThan(100);
  });

  it('nie pozwala zmienić działalności w cooldownie', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.turn = 1;
    state.company.lastActivityChangeTurn = 0;
    const result = applyChangeActivity(state, 'it');
    expect(result.company.activity).toBe('handel');
  });
});

describe('applyJoinParty / applyLeaveParty', () => {
  // Testy dla partii przenosimy do 1990 roku, bo w 1989 jest blokada
  it('dołączenie do partii ustawia currentPartyId', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1990; // <-- Zmieniamy rok!
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBe('sld');
  });

  it('dołączenie do partii zwiększa licznik przefarbowań', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1990; // <-- Zmieniamy rok!
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase.chaos).toBe(1);
  });

  it('nie pozwala przekroczyć limitu przefarbowań', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1990; // <-- Zmieniamy rok!
    state.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase.chaos = MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE;
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBeNull();
  });

  it('nie pozwala dołączyć do partii w 1989 roku', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1989;
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBeNull();
  });

  it('opuszczenie partii ustawia currentPartyId na null', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.ceo.currentPartyId = 'sld';
    const result = applyLeaveParty(state);
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('applyEmergencyLoan', () => {
  it('dodaje 50 000 PLN do konta', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = -10000; // <-- Ustawiamy ujemne saldo!
    const before = state.company.finances.stanKonta;
    const result = applyEmergencyLoan(state);
    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(before + 50_000);
  });

  it('resetuje bankruptWarningTurns', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = -10000; // <-- Ujemne saldo!
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = 2;
    const result = applyEmergencyLoan(state);
    expect(result.bankruptWarningTurns).toBe(0);
  });
});
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\engine\__tests__\competition.test.ts ===
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from './initialState';
import {
  generateCompetitors,
  tickCompetitors,
  computeRanking,
  interactWithCompetitor,
  ensureCompetitors,
  getCategoryId,
  SIZE_ORDER,
  SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR,
  INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN,
} from './competition';
import type { GameState } from './types';
import competitionData from '../data/competition.json';
import branchesData from '../data/branches.json';

const SIZES = new Set<string>(SIZE_ORDER);

function newState(seed = 'test-seed', activity = 'handel_odziez'): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed,
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity,
    companyName: 'Testex',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
  });
}

/** Symuluje upływ czasu: turn++, kwartały, lata — bez reszty silnika. */
function advance(s: GameState, ticks: number) {
  for (let i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
    s.turn += 1;
    const total = s.year * 4 + (s.quarter - 1) + 1;
    s.year = Math.floor(total / 4);
    s.quarter = ((total % 4) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
    tickCompetitors(s, 0);
  }
}

describe('data/competition.json', () => {
  const categoryIds = new Set((branchesData as { id: string }[]).map((c) => c.id));
  const templates = competitionData as any[];

  it('ma unikalne id i wymagane pola', () => {
    const ids = templates.map((t) => t.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const t of templates) {
      expect(typeof t.name).toBe('string');
      expect(typeof t.description).toBe('string');
    }
  });

  it('kategorie, jeśli podane, istnieją w branches.json', () => {
    for (const t of templates) {
      if (t.categories) for (const c of t.categories) expect(c === '*' || categoryIds.has(c)).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});

describe('generateCompetitors', () => {
  it('jest deterministyczne dla tego samego seeda', () => {
    expect(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'abc')).toEqual(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'abc'));
  });

  it('różne seedy dają różne statystyki', () => {
    const a = JSON.stringify(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'seed-a'));
    const b = JSON.stringify(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'seed-b'));
    expect(a).not.toBe(b);
  });

  it('zwraca unikalne firmy', () => {
    const list = generateCompetitors('produkcja', 1989, 'xyz');
    expect(new Set(list.map((c) => c.id)).size).toBe(list.length);
  });

  it('rozmiary: jedna duża, dwie średnie, reszta małe (gdy jest dość firm)', () => {
    const list = generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'mix');
    if (list.length < 3) return;
    const count = (size: string) => list.filter((c) => c.size === size).length;
    expect(count('large')).toBe(1);
    expect(count('medium')).toBe(Math.min(2, list.length - 1));
    expect(count('small')).toBe(Math.max(0, list.length - 3));
  });

  it('wartość mieści się w przedziale rozmiaru, a statystyki w 0-100', () => {
    for (const c of generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'ranges')) {
      const idx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(c.size);
      expect(c.value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[c.size] || 1000);
      if (SIZE_ORDER[idx + 1]) expect(c.value).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[SIZE_ORDER[idx + 1]]);
      for (const v of [c.reputation, c.condition, c.opinionOfPlayer]) {
        expect(v).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(v).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe('tickCompetitors', () => {
  it('jest deterministyczne dla tego samego seeda', () => {
    const a = newState('same');
    const b = newState('same');
    advance(a, 40);
    advance(b, 40);
    expect(a.competitors).toEqual(b.competitors);
  });

  it('utrzymuje statystyki w granicach przez 160 tur', () => {
    const s = newState('bounds');
    advance(s, 160);
    for (const c of s.competitors) {
      for (const v of [c.reputation, c.condition, c.opinionOfPlayer]) {
        expect(v).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(v).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
      }
      expect(c.value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1000);
      expect(SIZES.has(c.size)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('regeneruje konkurencję po zmianie kategorii działalności', () => {
    const s = newState('switch');
    s.company.activity = 'gast_kebab';
    advance(s, 1);
    expect(s.competitionCategory).toBe('gastronomia');
  });
});

describe('computeRanking', () => {
  it('sortuje malejąco, numeruje od 1 i zawiera gracza dokładnie raz', () => {
    const s = newState('rank');
    advance(s, 20);
    const rows = computeRanking(s);
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.isPlayer)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(s.competitors.length + 1);
    rows.forEach((r, i) => expect(r.rank).toBe(i + 1));
    for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) expect(rows[i - 1].score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(rows[i].score);
  });
});

  it('zużywa punkt interakcji i nie mutuje starego stanu', () => {
    const s = newState('interact');
    const id = s.competitors[0].id;
    const next = interactWithCompetitor(s, id, 'positive');
    expect(next.interactionPoints).toBe(s.interactionPoints - 1);
    expect(next).not.toBe(s);
    expect(s.interactionPoints).toBe(INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN);
  });

  it('bez punktów albo dla nieznanego id zwraca ten sam obiekt', () => {
    const s = newState('nopoints');
    s.interactionPoints = 0;
    expect(interactWithCompetitor(s, s.competitors[0].id, 'positive')).toBe(s);
    const s2 = newState('unknown');
    expect(interactWithCompetitor(s2, 'nie_ma_takiego', 'negative')).toBe(s2);
  });
});

describe('ensureCompetitors (migracja zapisu)', () => {
  it('uzupełnia brakujące pola w starym zapisie', () => {
    const s = newState('migrate') as any;
    delete s.competitors;
    delete s.competitionCategory;
    const migrated = ensureCompetitors(s);
    expect(Array.isArray(migrated.competitors)).toBe(true);
    expect(migrated.competitionCategory).toBe(getCategoryId('handel_odziez'));
  });

  it('nie rusza stanu, który już ma konkurentów', () => {
    const s = newState('nochange');
    expect(ensureCompetitors(s)).toBe(s);
  });
});

    companyName: 'Test',

describe('recomputeReputation', () => {
  it('zwraca obiekt z polem ogolna', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);
    expect(rep).toHaveProperty('ogolna');
    expect(typeof rep.ogolna).toBe('number');
  });

  it('ogolna mieści się w zakresie 0-100', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);
    expect(rep.ogolna).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(rep.ogolna).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
  });

  it('bardzo wysoka opinia mafii szkodzi opinii publicznej', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia = MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD + 20;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaMedia = 50;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy = 50;

    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);

    // Opinia publiczna nie może być wyższa niż przed kaskadą mafii
    // (dokładna wartość zależy od wag, ale spadek musi być)
    expect(rep.opiniaPubliczna).toBeLessThan(80);
  });

  it('klamruje wartości do 0-100', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = 150;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = -50;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy = 200;

    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);

    for (const key of ['branza', 'opiniaPubliczna', 'opiniaPracownicy', 'opiniaBranza', 'opiniaMafia', 'opiniaMedia', 'opiniaPolitycy', 'ogolna'] as const) {
      expect(rep[key]).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(rep[key]).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
    }
  });

  it('nie modyfikuje oryginalnego stanu (immutability)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const originalRep = { ...state.company.reputation };
    recomputeReputation(state);
    expect(state.company.reputation).toEqual(originalRep);
  });
});

    expect(rng1.next()).not.toBe(rng2.next());
  });

  it('int zwraca liczby w podanym zakresie', () => {
    const rng = makeRng('test', 1);
    for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
      const value = rng.int(1, 10);
      expect(value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(value).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);

=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\engine\actions.ts ===
import type { GameState, ContractType, Pace, EventEffects, CompanySize, NewsItem} from './types';
import {
  MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE,
  ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN,
  BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN,
  WORKER_CONFIG,
  COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG,
  BRANCH_COST,
  MAX_NEWS
} from './constants';
import { getActivityData } from './activityHelper';
import { recomputeReputation } from './reputation';
import { makeRng } from './rng';


const clamp = (x: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, x));

export function applyHire(state: GameState, contract: ContractType, delta: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const current = s.company.employees[contract];
  const newCount = Math.max(0, current + delta);
  const actualDelta = newCount - current;

  if (actualDelta < 0 && contract === 'uop') {
    const fired = Math.abs(actualDelta);
    const severance = fired * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.severance;
    if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < severance) {
      return s;
    }
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= severance;
  }

  if (actualDelta < 0 && contract !== 'uop') {
    const fired = Math.abs(actualDelta);
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy = Math.max(
      0,
      s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy - fired * 2
    );
  }

  if (actualDelta > 0) {
    const hired = actualDelta;
    const repImpact = WORKER_CONFIG[contract].reputationImpact;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy = clamp(
      s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy + repImpact * hired
    );
  }

  s.company.employees[contract] = newCount;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  return s;
}

export function applyLoan(state: GameState, source: 'bank' | 'szara_strefa', amount: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const rate = source === 'bank' ? 0.03 : 0.07;
  s.company.loans.push({
    id: `loan_${s.turn}_${s.company.loans.length}`,
    source,
    principal: amount,
    remaining: amount,
    interestRate: rate,
    quartersLeft: 12,
  });
  s.company.finances.stanKonta += amount;
  if (source === 'bank') s.company.finances.wplywy.srodkiZKredytow += amount;
  else s.company.finances.wplywy.dofinansowaniaSzaraStrefa += amount;
  return s;
}

export function applyChangeActivity(state: GameState, activity: string): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (s.turn - s.company.lastActivityChangeTurn < ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN) return s;
  
  const oldBranza = s.company.reputation.branza;
  const actData = getActivityData(activity); 
  
  s.company.activity = activity;
  s.company.reputation.branza = actData?.rep ?? 50;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaMafia = actData?.mafia ?? 25; 
  s.company.lastActivityChangeTurn = s.turn;
  
  const delta = s.company.reputation.branza - oldBranza;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + delta * 0.3);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  
  return s;
}

export function applyJoinParty(state: GameState, partyId: string): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (s.year <= 1989) return s;
  if (s.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[s.phase] >= MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE) return s;
  s.ceo.currentPartyId = partyId;
  s.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[s.phase] += 1;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy - 5);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  return s;
}

export function applyLeaveParty(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  s.ceo.currentPartyId = null;
  return s;
}

export function applyEmergencyLoan(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta > 0) return s; // Kredyt tylko gdy jesteśmy pod wodą
  s.company.loans.push({
    id: `emergency_${s.turn}`,
    source: 'bank',
    principal: BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.amount,
    remaining: BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.amount,
    interestRate: BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.interestRate,
    quartersLeft: BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.quartersLeft,
  });
  s.company.finances.stanKonta += BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.amount;
  s.bankruptWarningTurns = 0;
  return s;
}

export function applySetPace(state: GameState, pace: Pace): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  s.pace = pace;
  return s;
}

export function buyOfficeItem(state: GameState, itemId: string, price: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < price) return s; // Brak kasy
  if (s.company.inventory.includes(itemId)) return s; // Już to masz
  
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= price;
  s.company.inventory.push(itemId);
  return s;
}

export function applyEffects(state: GameState, e: EventEffects) {
  if (e.reputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.reputation)) {
      (state.company.reputation as any)[k] = clamp((state.company.reputation as any)[k] + (v as number));
    }
  }
  if (e.stanKonta) state.company.finances.stanKonta += e.stanKonta;
  if (e.wplywy) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wplywy)) {
    (state.company.finances.wplywy as any)[k] += v as number;
  }
  if (e.wydatki) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wydatki)) {
    (state.company.finances.wydatki as any)[k] += v as number;
  }
  if (e.flagsAdd) state.ceo.flags.push(...e.flagsAdd);
  if (e.flagsRemove) state.ceo.flags = state.ceo.flags.filter(f => !e.flagsRemove!.includes(f));
  if (e.joinMafia) state.ceo.inMafia = true;
  if (e.leaveMafia) state.ceo.inMafia = false;
  if (e.setParty) state.ceo.party = e.setParty;
  if (e.setCurrentPartyId) state.ceo.currentPartyId = e.setCurrentPartyId;
  if (e.divideFinances) {
    const div = (n: number) => Math.round((n / 10000) * 100) / 100;
    
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = div(state.company.finances.stanKonta);
    
    const wplywy = (state.company.finances as any).wplywy || (state.company.finances as any).aktywa;
    if (wplywy) {
      for (const k in wplywy) wplywy[k] = div(wplywy[k]);
    }
    
    const wydatki = (state.company.finances as any).wydatki || (state.company.finances as any).pasywa;
    if (wydatki) {
      for (const k in wydatki) wydatki[k] = div(wydatki[k]);
    }

    for (const loan of state.company.loans) {
      loan.principal = div(loan.principal);
      loan.remaining = div(loan.remaining);
    }
  }
  if (e.employeesDelta) {
    if (e.employeesDelta.bezUmowy) state.company.employees.bezUmowy = Math.max(0, state.company.employees.bezUmowy + e.employeesDelta.bezUmowy);
    if (e.employeesDelta.zlecenie) state.company.employees.zlecenie = Math.max(0, state.company.employees.zlecenie + e.employeesDelta.zlecenie);
    if (e.employeesDelta.uop) state.company.employees.uop = Math.max(0, state.company.employees.uop + e.employeesDelta.uop);
  }
   if (e.addItem && !state.company.inventory.includes(e.addItem)) {
    state.company.inventory.push(e.addItem);
  }   
}

export function toggleAccountant(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  s.company.hasAccountant = !s.company.hasAccountant;
  return s;
}

export function changeCompanySize(state: GameState, direction: 'up' | 'down'): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const sizes: CompanySize[] = ['micro', 'small', 'medium', 'large', 'very_large', 'corporation'];
  const currentIndex = sizes.indexOf(s.company.size);
  
  let newIndex = currentIndex;
  if (direction === 'up' && currentIndex < sizes.length - 1) newIndex++;
  if (direction === 'down' && currentIndex > 0) newIndex--;
  if (newIndex === currentIndex) return s;

  const newSize = sizes[newIndex];
  const config = COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG[newSize];
  
  if (direction === 'up') {
    if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < config.cost) return s;
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= config.cost;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna += config.repBonus;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy += config.repBonus;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza += config.repBonus;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia += config.repBonus;
  } else {
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna += config.repPenalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy += config.repPenalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza += config.repPenalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia += config.repPenalty;
  }
  
  s.company.size = newSize;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  return s;
}

export function buildBranch(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (!['large', 'very_large', 'corporation'].includes(s.company.size)) return s;
  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < BRANCH_COST) return s;
  
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= BRANCH_COST;
  s.company.branches += 1;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza += 5;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  return s;
}

export function buyResource(state: GameState, resourceId: string, amount: number, basePrice: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (amount <= 0) return s; // Blokada ujemnych ilości
  
  const discount = Math.min(0.4, (s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia) / 500);
  const totalCost = Math.round(amount * basePrice * (1 - discount));
  
  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < totalCost) return s;
  
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= totalCost;
  s.company.resources[resourceId] = (s.company.resources[resourceId] || 0) + amount;
  return s;
}

export function sellResource(state: GameState, resourceId: string, amount: number, basePrice: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (amount <= 0) return s;
  
  const available = s.company.resources[resourceId] || 0;
  if (available < amount) return s;
  
  const bonus = Math.min(0.8, (s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia) / 250);
  const totalRevenue = Math.round(amount * basePrice * 0.8 * (1 + bonus));
  
  s.company.finances.stanKonta += totalRevenue;
  s.company.resources[resourceId] -= amount;
  
  if (s.company.activity.includes('kult_')) {
    s.company.audience += amount * 5; 
  }
  return s;
}

export function buyAd(state: GameState, type: 'radio' | 'tv' | 'internet'): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  let cost = 0;
  let repGain = 0;
  let mediaGain = 0;

  if (type === 'radio') { cost = 5000; repGain = 2; mediaGain = 2; }
  if (type === 'tv') { cost = 50000; repGain = 8; mediaGain = 10; }
  if (type === 'internet') { cost = 15000; repGain = 4; mediaGain = 5; }

  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < cost) return s;

  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= cost;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna + repGain);
  s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia + mediaGain);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  
  return s;
}

export function buyGoods(state: GameState, supplier: any, amount: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);

  const currentStock = Object.values(s.company.resources).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  if (currentStock + amount > s.company.warehouseCapacity) return s;

  // Obliczanie ceny hurtowej (im więcej, tym taniej)
  let discount = 0;
  if (amount === 10) discount = 0.1; // 10% taniej
  if (amount === 100) discount = 0.25; // 25% taniej

  const totalCost = Math.round(supplier.price * amount * (1 - discount));
  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < totalCost) return s;

  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= totalCost;
  const resId = `${supplier.id}_${supplier.quality}`;
  s.company.resources[resId] = (s.company.resources[resId] || 0) + amount;
  
  return s;
}

export function sellGoods(state: GameState, buyer: any, resId: string, amount: number, baseValue: number): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const available = s.company.resources[resId] || 0;
  if (available < amount) return s;

  // Zabieramy towar z magazynu od razu
  s.company.resources[resId] -= amount;

  const parts = resId.split('_');
  const quality = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1]) || 1;
  const estimatedRevenue = Math.round(baseValue * buyer.multiplier * amount);

  // Dodajemy do kolejki sprzedaży (rozliczy się w następnej turze)
  s.pendingSales.push({
    id: `sale_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
    buyerName: buyer.name,
    resId,
    amount,
    estimatedRevenue,
    quality,
    hasComplaints: buyer.complaints,
    installmentMonths: 0,
    finalRevenue: 0
  });

  return s;
}

export function rentWarehouse(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const cost = 10000;
  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < cost) return s;
  
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= cost;
  s.company.warehouseCapacity += 50;
  return s;
}

export function resolvePendingSales(state: GameState): void {
  if (state.pendingSales.length === 0) return;

  let totalRevenue = 0;

  for (const sale of state.pendingSales) {
    const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 13 + 9);
    const variance = 0.9 + (rng.next() * 0.2);
    const actualRevenue = Math.round(sale.estimatedRevenue * variance);
    totalRevenue += actualRevenue;
    
    state.company.finances.stanKonta += actualRevenue;

    // Szansa na reklamację
    if (sale.hasComplaints && sale.quality < 5) {
      const chance = 0.6 - (sale.quality * 0.1);
      if (rng.next() < chance) {
        state.pendingComplaints.push({
          id: `comp_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
          itemName: sale.resId.split('_')[0],
          buyerName: sale.buyerName,
          penalty: 10 - sale.quality
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // Generowanie wiadomości z podsumowaniem
  if (totalRevenue > 0) {
    const newsItem: NewsItem = {
      id: `sales_report_${state.turn}_${Date.now()}`,
      turn: state.turn,
      year: state.year,
      quarter: state.quarter,
      headline: 'Rozliczenie sprzedaży',
      body: `Kontrahenci uiścili płatności za dostarczone towary. Na Twoje konto wpłynęło ${totalRevenue.toLocaleString('pl-PL')} PLN.`,
      category: 'economy',
      read: false
    };
    state.news.unshift(newsItem);
    if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
  }

  state.pendingSales = []; // Czyścimy kolejkę
}

export function resolveComplaint(state: GameState, id: string, accept: boolean): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const comp = s.pendingComplaints.find(c => c.id === id);
  if (!comp) return s;

  if (accept) {
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= 5000; // Koszt przyjęcia zwrotu
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna += 2; // Lekki plus za uczciwość
  } else {
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna -= comp.penalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza -= comp.penalty;
  }

  s.pendingComplaints = s.pendingComplaints.filter(c => c.id !== id);
  return s;
}

// Akcje wobec konkurencji
export function interactWithCompetitor(state: GameState, compId: string, actionType: 'positive' | 'negative'): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  if (s.interactionPoints <= 0) return s;

  s.interactionPoints -= 1;

  if (actionType === 'positive') {
    // Np. wysłanie kwiatów, współpraca
    s.competitorsReputation[compId] = Math.min(100, s.competitorsReputation[compId] + 5);
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = Math.min(100, s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + 2);
  } else {
    // Np. podłożenie świni (50% szans na sukces)
    const rng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn * 100 + compId.length);
    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
      s.competitorsReputation[compId] = Math.max(0, s.competitorsReputation[compId] - 10);
      s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = Math.min(100, s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + 3); // Zyskujesz na ich stracie
    } else {
      // Wpadłeś
      s.competitorsReputation[compId] = Math.max(0, s.competitorsReputation[compId] - 5);
      s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = Math.max(0, s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza - 5);
      s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = Math.max(0, s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna - 5);
    }
  }

  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  return s;
}

// Logika płatności ratalnej
export function calculateInstallmentPrice(price: number, months: number): number {
  if (months <= 1) return price;
  // Im więcej rat, tym wyższe odsetki (np. 5% na miesiąc)
  const interest = 1 + (months * 0.05);
  return Math.round(price * interest);
}

import type { GameState, Point } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import { applyEffects } from './actions';
import { recomputeReputation } from './reputation';

export function startChase(state: GameState): void {
  const size = 8; // Plansza 8x8
  const grid = Array(size).fill(null).map(() => Array(size).fill(0));
  
  // Startujemy w lewym górnym rogu i od razu go zamalowujemy
  grid[0][0] = 1; 

  state.activeChase = {
    player: { x: 0, y: 0 },
    enemies: [
      { x: 7, y: 7 }, // Przeciwnicy startują w przeciwległych rogach
      { x: 7, y: 0 },
    ],
    gridSize: size,
    grid,
    turn: 1,
    maxTurns: 30, // Dajemy więcej tur, bo zamalowanie wymaga czasu
    finished: false,
    result: null,
  };
}

export function movePlayer(state: GameState, dx: number, dy: number): void {
  const c = state.activeChase;
  if (!c || c.finished) return;

  const newX = c.player.x + dx;
  const newY = c.player.y + dy;

  // Sprawdzenie granic planszy
  if (newX < 0 || newX >= c.gridSize || newY < 0 || newY >= c.gridSize) return;

  // Gracz się porusza i zamalowuje kafelek
  c.player = { x: newX, y: newY };
  c.grid[newY][newX] = 1;

  // Sprawdzenie czy gracz wygrał (np. 40% planszy zamalowane)
  let paintedCount = 0;
  for (let y = 0; y < c.gridSize; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < c.gridSize; x++) {
      if (c.grid[y][x] === 1) paintedCount++;
    }
  }
  const totalTiles = c.gridSize * c.gridSize;
  if (paintedCount / totalTiles >= 0.4) {
    endChase(state, 'escaped');
    return;
  }

  // Ruch wrogów (idą w stronę gracza, ale tylko po pustych polach)
  c.enemies = c.enemies.map(enemy => {
    let ex = enemy.x;
    let ey = enemy.y;

    const possibleMoves = [
      { x: ex + 1, y: ey },
      { x: ex - 1, y: ey },
      { x: ex, y: ey + 1 },
      { x: ex, y: ey - 1 }
    ].filter(p => 
      p.x >= 0 && p.x < c.gridSize && 
      p.y >= 0 && p.y < c.gridSize && 
      c.grid[p.y][p.x] === 0 // Tylko puste pola
    );

    if (possibleMoves.length > 0) {
      // Wybierz ruch, który przybliża do gracza
      possibleMoves.sort((a, b) => {
        const distA = Math.abs(a.x - c.player.x) + Math.abs(a.y - c.player.y);
        const distB = Math.abs(b.x - c.player.x) + Math.abs(b.y - c.player.y);
        return distA - distB;
      });
      return possibleMoves[0];
    }
    return enemy;
  });

  // Sprawdzenie kolizji
  const caught = c.enemies.some(e => e.x === c.player.x && e.y === c.player.y);
  if (caught) {
    endChase(state, 'caught');
    return;
  }

  c.turn += 1;
  if (c.turn > c.maxTurns) {
    endChase(state, 'caught'); // Czas minął, a oni cię dopadli
  }
}

function endChase(state: GameState, result: 'escaped' | 'caught') {
  const c = state.activeChase;
  if (!c) return;

  c.finished = true;
  c.result = result;

  if (result === 'escaped') {
    applyEffects(state, {
      stanKonta: -10000,
      reputation: { opiniaPubliczna: 10, opiniaMafia: -10 },
    });
  } else {
    applyEffects(state, {
      stanKonta: -50000,
      reputation: { opiniaPubliczna: -15, opiniaMafia: -20 },
    });
  }
  
  state.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(state);
}

export function dismissChase(state: GameState): void {
  state.activeChase = null;
}

import type { GameState } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import {
  COMBAT_SKARBOWKA_PLAYER_HP,
  COMBAT_SKARBOWKA_ENEMY_HP,
  COMBAT_MAX_ROUNDS,
} from './constants';
import { applyEffects } from './actions';
import { recomputeReputation } from './reputation';

export type CombatMove = 'wspolpraca' | 'adwokat' | 'klamstwo' | 'wplywy' | 'mafia';

export function startTaxCombat(state: GameState): void {
  state.activeCombat = {
    type: 'skarbowka',
    playerHp: COMBAT_SKARBOWKA_PLAYER_HP,
    playerHpMax: COMBAT_SKARBOWKA_PLAYER_HP,
    enemyHp: COMBAT_SKARBOWKA_ENEMY_HP,
    enemyHpMax: COMBAT_SKARBOWKA_ENEMY_HP,
    round: 1,
    log: [
      { text: 'Urzędnik skarbowy rozkłada teczkę z dokumentami.', type: 'system' },
      { text: 'Zaczyna się kontrola.', type: 'enemy' },
    ],
    finished: false,
    result: null,
  };
}

export function executeCombatMove(state: GameState, move: CombatMove): void {
  const c = state.activeCombat;
  if (!c || c.finished) return;

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 1000 + c.round);
  let playerDamage = 0;
  let playerMsg = '';

  // --- RUCH GRACZA ---
  switch (move) {
    case 'wspolpraca':
      playerDamage = 10;
      playerMsg = 'Zapewniasz o pełnej współpracy. Urzędnik kiwa głową.';
      break;
    case 'adwokat':
      if (state.company.finances.stanKonta >= 20000) {
        state.company.finances.stanKonta -= 20000;
        playerDamage = 25;
        playerMsg = 'Twój adwokat przejmuje rozmowę. Powołuje się na paragrafy. Urzędnik blednie. (-20 000 PLN)';
      } else {
        playerMsg = 'Chcesz wezwać adwokata, ale nie masz na to pieniędzy! Tracisz szansę.';
        playerDamage = 0;
      }
      break;
    case 'klamstwo':
      playerDamage = 30;
      if (rng.next() < 0.3) {
        const selfDamage = 20;
        c.playerHp = Math.max(0, c.playerHp - selfDamage);
        playerMsg = `Kłamiesz, ale urzędnik od razu to wyłapuje. Tracisz ${selfDamage} siły argumentów.`;
      } else {
        playerMsg = 'Mijasz się z prawdą, ale udaje ci się zbić urzędnika z tropu.';
      }
      break;
    case 'wplywy':
      if (state.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy > 60) {
        playerDamage = 40;
        playerMsg = 'Dzwonisz do znajomego polityka. Urzędnik dostaje telefon z samej góry.';
      } else {
        playerMsg = 'Próbujesz powołać się na wpływy, ale nikt cię nie zna. Zero efektu.';
        playerDamage = 0;
      }
      break;
    case 'mafia':
      if (state.ceo.inMafia) {
        playerDamage = 50;
        playerMsg = 'Dzwonisz do "chłopaków". Urzędnik nagle przypomina sobie o braku dowodów i szybko kończy kontrolę.';
      } else {
        playerMsg = 'Nie masz do kogo zadzwonić.';
        playerDamage = 0;
      }
      break;      
  }

  c.enemyHp = Math.max(0, c.enemyHp - playerDamage);
  if (playerMsg) c.log.push({ text: playerMsg, type: 'player' });

  if (c.enemyHp <= 0) {
    endCombat(state, 'win');
    return;
  }

  // --- RUCH WROGA ---
  const enemyRoll = rng.next();
  let enemyDamage = 10;
  let enemyMsg = 'Urzędnik pyta o brakujące dokumenty.';

  if (enemyRoll > 0.7) {
    enemyDamage = 20;
    enemyMsg = 'Urzędnik nakłada na ciebie grzywnę za opóźnienia.';
  } else if (enemyRoll > 0.4) {
    enemyDamage = 15;
    enemyMsg = 'Urzędnik znajduje nieprawidłowości w sprawozdaniu.';
  }

  c.playerHp = Math.max(0, c.playerHp - enemyDamage);
  c.log.push({ text: `${enemyMsg} (-${enemyDamage} siły)`, type: 'enemy' });

  if (c.playerHp <= 0) {
    endCombat(state, 'lose');
    return;
  }

  // --- KONIEC RUNDY ---
  c.round += 1;
  if (c.round > COMBAT_MAX_ROUNDS) {
    endCombat(state, 'draw');
  }
}

function endCombat(state: GameState, result: 'win' | 'lose' | 'draw') {
  const c = state.activeCombat;
  if (!c) return;

  c.finished = true;
  c.result = result;

  if (result === 'win') {
    c.log.push({ text: 'Urzędnik zamyka teczkę. "Tym razem się wywinąłeś".', type: 'system' });
    applyEffects(state, { reputation: { opiniaSkarbowa: 15 } });
  } else if (result === 'lose') {
    c.log.push({ text: 'Urzędnik dopina swego. Wystawia mandat na 50 000 PLN.', type: 'system' });
    applyEffects(state, { stanKonta: -50000, reputation: { opiniaPubliczna: -10, opiniaSkarbowa: 10 } });
  } else {
    c.log.push({ text: 'Urzędnik jest zmęczony, ale znajduje błędy. Mandat 20 000 PLN.', type: 'system' });
    applyEffects(state, { stanKonta: -20000, reputation: { opiniaSkarbowa: 5 } });
  }

  state.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(state);
  state.skarbowkaVisitTurn = null; // Zakończ wizytę
}

export function dismissCombat(state: GameState): void {
  state.activeCombat = null;
}

import type {
  GameState,
  Competitor,
  CompanySize,
  CompetitorTrend,
  PlayerSnapshot,
  Trend,
} from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import { recomputeReputation } from './reputation';
import competitionData from '../data/competition.json';
import branchesData from '../data/branches.json';

// ===========================================================================
// KONFIGURACJA — wszystkie liczby do ustawienia po Twojej stronie.
// Wartości poniżej to tylko techniczne placeholdery, żeby kod działał.
// ===========================================================================

/** Ile interakcji z konkurencją gracz ma na turę. */
export const INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN = 2;

/** Domyślne wartości dla pól filtrujących, których nie ma we wpisie w data/competition.json. */
const TEMPLATE_DEFAULTS = {
  categories: ['*'] as string[], // "*" = firma jest konkurentem w każdej branży
  startYear: 1989,
};

/**
 * Losowy podział rozmiarów konkurencji: pierwsze sloty to wskazane rozmiary,
 * reszta firm dostaje DEFAULT_COMPETITOR_SIZE. Które firmy dostaną który rozmiar — losowo (seed).
 */
export const COMPETITOR_SIZE_MIX: CompanySize[] = ['large', 'medium', 'medium'];
export const DEFAULT_COMPETITOR_SIZE: CompanySize = 'small';

/** Zakresy losowania statystyk startowych (włącznie). Wartość firmy wynika z jej rozmiaru. */
const GENERATION = {
  reputation: { min: 20, max: 80 },
  condition: { min: 30, max: 70 },
  opinion: { min: 30, max: 70 },
};

/** Dynamika zmian na kwartał. */
const TUNING = {
  conditionPull: 0.15, // jak mocno kondycja wraca do bazowej (0-1)
  conditionNoise: 6, // losowy szum kondycji: ±
  reputationPull: 0.1,
  reputationNoise: 3,
  opinionPull: 0.1,
  opinionNoise: 1,
  valueGrowthFromCondition: 0.04, // maks. wzrost wartości na kwartał z samej kondycji
  valueNoise: 0.06, // całkowita rozpiętość losowego szumu wartości
  trendMinDelta: 2, // min. zmiana reputacji/kondycji, by pokazać trend
  trendMinRatio: 0.015, // min. zmiana wartości, by pokazać trend
};

/** Skutki interakcji gracza z konkurentem (wartości z Twojej oryginalnej wersji). */
export const INTERACTION_EFFECTS = {
  positive: {
    competitorReputation: 5,
    competitorOpinion: 0, // <-- do ustawienia
    playerOpiniaBranza: 2,
  },
  negativeSuccess: {
    chance: 0.5,
    competitorReputation: -10,
    competitorOpinion: 0, // <-- do ustawienia
    playerOpiniaBranza: 3,
  },
  negativeFail: {
    competitorReputation: -5,
    competitorOpinion: 0, // <-- do ustawienia
    playerOpiniaBranza: -5,
    playerOpiniaPubliczna: -5,
  },
};

export const SIZE_ORDER: CompanySize[] = [
  'micro',
  'small',
  'medium',
  'large',
  'very_large',
  'corporation',
];

/** Wartość (PLN), od której firma awansuje do danego rozmiaru. */
export const SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR: Record<CompanySize, number> = {
  micro: 0,
  small: 300_000,
  medium: 2_000_000,
  large: 15_000_000,
  very_large: 100_000_000,
  corporation: 600_000_000,
};
/** Spadek rozmiaru dopiero poniżej tego ułamka progu (histereza). */
const SIZE_DOWNGRADE_RATIO = 0.7;

/** Wagi w wyniku rankingowym (suma = 1). */
export const RANKING_WEIGHTS = { value: 0.35, size: 0.25, reputation: 0.2, condition: 0.2 } as const;

// ===========================================================================
// Szablony (data/competition.json)
// ===========================================================================

/**
 * Wpis z data/competition.json. Wymagane: id, name, description.
 * Statystyki są losowane przy tworzeniu gry — `baseReputation` z JSON jest ignorowane.
 */
export interface CompetitorTemplate {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  baseReputation?: number; // przestarzałe, nieużywane
  /** Id kategorii z branches.json albo "*". */
  categories?: string[];
  startYear?: number;
}

const TEMPLATES = competitionData as unknown as CompetitorTemplate[];

interface BranchCategory {
  id: string;
  activities: { id: string }[];
}

/** Zwraca id kategorii (np. "handel") dla id działalności (np. "handel_odziez"). */
export function getCategoryId(activityId: string): string | null {
  const cat = (branchesData as BranchCategory[]).find((c) =>
    c.activities.some((a) => a.id === activityId),
  );
  return cat?.id ?? null;
}

function templatesFor(category: string, year: number): CompetitorTemplate[] {
  return TEMPLATES.filter((t) => {
    const cats = t.categories ?? TEMPLATE_DEFAULTS.categories;
    const start = t.startYear ?? TEMPLATE_DEFAULTS.startYear;
    return (cats.includes('*') || cats.includes(category)) && start <= year;
  });
}

// ===========================================================================
// Pomocnicze
// ===========================================================================

type Rng = ReturnType<typeof makeRng>;

const clamp = (v: number, min = 0, max = 100) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, v));

function trendDiff(cur: number, prev: number, minDelta: number): Trend {
  const d = cur - prev;
  return d >= minDelta ? 1 : d <= -minDelta ? -1 : 0;
}

function trendRatio(cur: number, prev: number, minRatio: number): Trend {
  if (prev <= 0) return 0;
  const r = cur / prev - 1;
  return r >= minRatio ? 1 : r <= -minRatio ? -1 : 0;
}

/** Rozmiar z histerezą: awans po przekroczeniu progu, spadek dopiero poniżej ułamka progu. */
function resolveSize(size: CompanySize, value: number): CompanySize {
  let idx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(size);
  while (idx < SIZE_ORDER.length - 1 && value >= SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[SIZE_ORDER[idx + 1]]) idx++;
  while (idx > 0 && value < SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[SIZE_ORDER[idx]] * SIZE_DOWNGRADE_RATIO) idx--;
  return SIZE_ORDER[idx];
}

// ===========================================================================
// Generowanie i migracja
// ===========================================================================

function shuffle<T>(arr: T[], rng: Rng): T[] {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = rng.int(0, i);
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
}

/** Wartość losowana logarytmicznie w przedziale [próg rozmiaru, próg następnego rozmiaru). */
function rollValue(size: CompanySize, rng: Rng): number {
  const idx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(size);
  const low = Math.max(SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[size], 1000);
  const next = SIZE_ORDER[idx + 1];
  const high = next ? SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[next] : low * 2;
  return Math.round(Math.exp(Math.log(low) + rng.next() * (Math.log(high) - Math.log(low))));
}

function createCompetitor(t: CompetitorTemplate, size: CompanySize, rng: Rng): Competitor {
  const reputation = rng.int(GENERATION.reputation.min, GENERATION.reputation.max);
  const condition = rng.int(GENERATION.condition.min, GENERATION.condition.max);
  const opinion = rng.int(GENERATION.opinion.min, GENERATION.opinion.max);
  return {
    id: t.id,
    name: t.name,
    description: t.description,
    size,
    reputation,
    value: rollValue(size, rng),
    condition,
    opinionOfPlayer: opinion,
    // Statystyki dryfują wokół wylosowanych wartości startowych.
    baseReputation: reputation,
    baseCondition: condition,
    baseOpinion: opinion,
    trend: { value: 0, reputation: 0, condition: 0 },
  };
}

/**
 * Konkurenci z szablonów pasujących do kategorii i roku, ze statystykami losowanymi
 * (deterministycznie dla seeda): rozmiary wg COMPETITOR_SIZE_MIX, reszta małe.
 */
export function generateCompetitors(category: string, year: number, seed: string): Competitor[] {
  const rng = makeRng(`${seed}:competition-init`, year);
  const pool = templatesFor(category, year);

  const sizeById = new Map<string, CompanySize>();
  shuffle(pool, rng).forEach((t, i) => {
    sizeById.set(t.id, COMPETITOR_SIZE_MIX[i] ?? DEFAULT_COMPETITOR_SIZE);
  });

  return pool.map((t) => createCompetitor(t, sizeById.get(t.id)!, rng));
}

/** Migracja starych zapisów (bez pola `competitors`). Nie mutuje wejścia. */
export function ensureCompetitors(s: GameState): GameState {
  if (Array.isArray(s.competitors) && typeof s.competitionCategory === 'string') return s;
  const category = getCategoryId(s.company.activity) ?? '';
  return {
    ...s,
    competitors: category ? generateCompetitors(category, s.year, s.seed) : [],
    competitionCategory: category,
    playerPrev: s.playerPrev ?? null,
    interactionPoints: s.interactionPoints ?? INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN,
  };
}

// ===========================================================================
// Tura
// ===========================================================================

function evolveCompetitor(c: Competitor, rng: Rng) {
  const before = { value: c.value, reputation: c.reputation, condition: c.condition };

  c.condition = clamp(
    c.condition +
      (c.baseCondition - c.condition) * TUNING.conditionPull +
      rng.int(-TUNING.conditionNoise, TUNING.conditionNoise),
  );
  c.reputation = clamp(
    c.reputation +
      (c.baseReputation - c.reputation) * TUNING.reputationPull +
      rng.int(-TUNING.reputationNoise, TUNING.reputationNoise),
  );
  c.opinionOfPlayer = clamp(
    c.opinionOfPlayer +
      (c.baseOpinion - c.opinionOfPlayer) * TUNING.opinionPull +
      rng.int(-TUNING.opinionNoise, TUNING.opinionNoise),
  );

  const growth =
    ((c.condition - 50) / 50) * TUNING.valueGrowthFromCondition + (rng.next() - 0.5) * TUNING.valueNoise;
  c.value = Math.max(1000, Math.round(c.value * (1 + growth)));
  c.size = resolveSize(c.size, c.value);

  const trend: CompetitorTrend = {
    value: trendRatio(c.value, before.value, TUNING.trendMinRatio),
    reputation: trendDiff(c.reputation, before.reputation, TUNING.trendMinDelta),
    condition: trendDiff(c.condition, before.condition, TUNING.trendMinDelta),
  };
  c.trend = trend;
}

/**
 * Jeden krok symulacji konkurencji (kwartał). MUTUJE przekazany stan
 * (tak jak applyEffects) — wołaj na klonie, np. wewnątrz nextTurn.
 */
export function tickCompetitors(s: GameState, step = 0): GameState {
  const category = getCategoryId(s.company.activity) ?? '';

  // Pierwsza tura po migracji albo zmiana branży przez gracza: konkurencja z nowej kategorii.
  if (!Array.isArray(s.competitors) || s.competitionCategory !== category) {
    s.competitors = category ? generateCompetitors(category, s.year, s.seed) : [];
    s.competitionCategory = category;
    return s;
  }

  const rng = makeRng(`${s.seed}:competition`, s.turn * 10 + step);
  for (const c of s.competitors) evolveCompetitor(c, rng);
  return s;
}

// ===========================================================================
// Interakcje gracza z konkurentem
// ===========================================================================

export function interactWithCompetitor(
  state: GameState,
  compId: string,
  actionType: 'positive' | 'negative',
): GameState {
  if (state.gameOver || state.interactionPoints <= 0) return state;
  const idx = (state.competitors ?? []).findIndex((c) => c.id === compId);
  if (idx < 0) return state;

  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const c = s.competitors[idx];
  const rep = s.company.reputation;
  s.interactionPoints -= 1;

  if (actionType === 'positive') {
    const fx = INTERACTION_EFFECTS.positive;
    c.reputation = clamp(c.reputation + fx.competitorReputation);
    c.opinionOfPlayer = clamp(c.opinionOfPlayer + fx.competitorOpinion);
    rep.opiniaBranza = clamp(rep.opiniaBranza + fx.playerOpiniaBranza);
  } else {
    // Sól zależy od tury, konkurenta i liczby pozostałych punktów — każdy rzut jest inny.
    const rng = makeRng(`${s.seed}:interact`, s.turn * 100 + idx * 7 + s.interactionPoints);
    if (rng.next() < INTERACTION_EFFECTS.negativeSuccess.chance) {
      const fx = INTERACTION_EFFECTS.negativeSuccess;
      c.reputation = clamp(c.reputation + fx.competitorReputation);
      c.opinionOfPlayer = clamp(c.opinionOfPlayer + fx.competitorOpinion);
      rep.opiniaBranza = clamp(rep.opiniaBranza + fx.playerOpiniaBranza);
    } else {
      const fx = INTERACTION_EFFECTS.negativeFail;
      c.reputation = clamp(c.reputation + fx.competitorReputation);
      c.opinionOfPlayer = clamp(c.opinionOfPlayer + fx.competitorOpinion);
      rep.opiniaBranza = clamp(rep.opiniaBranza + fx.playerOpiniaBranza);
      rep.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(rep.opiniaPubliczna + fx.playerOpiniaPubliczna);
    }
  }

  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);
  return s;
}

// ===========================================================================
// Ranking
// ===========================================================================

export interface RankingRow {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  isPlayer: boolean;
  rank: number;
  score: number;
  size: CompanySize;
  reputation: number;
  value: number;
  condition: number;
  /** null dla gracza. */
  opinionOfPlayer: number | null;
  trend: CompetitorTrend;
}

export function computePlayerValue(s: GameState): number {
  const c = s.company;
  const debts = c.loans.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.remaining, 0);
  return Math.max(0, Math.round(c.finances.stanKonta - debts));
}

export function rankingScore(size: CompanySize, value: number, reputation: number, condition: number): number {
  const valueScore = clamp(((Math.log10(Math.max(value, 1)) - 4) / 5) * 100);
  const sizeScore = (SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(size) / (SIZE_ORDER.length - 1)) * 100;
  const score =
    valueScore * RANKING_WEIGHTS.value +
    sizeScore * RANKING_WEIGHTS.size +
    clamp(reputation) * RANKING_WEIGHTS.reputation +
    clamp(condition) * RANKING_WEIGHTS.condition;
  return Math.round(score * 10) / 10;
}

/** Gracz i konkurenci, od najwyżej ocenionej firmy. */
export function computeRanking(s: GameState): RankingRow[] {
  const prev = s.playerPrev ?? null;

  const pValue = computePlayerValue(s);
  const pRep = s.company.reputation.ogolna;
  const pCond = s.profitabilityScore;
  const pTrendCond: Trend =
    s.profitabilityTrend === 'up1' || s.profitabilityTrend === 'up2'
      ? 1
      : s.profitabilityTrend === 'down1' || s.profitabilityTrend === 'down2'
        ? -1
        : 0;

  const rows: RankingRow[] = [
    {
      id: 'player',
      name: `${s.company.name}${s.company.suffix}`,
      isPlayer: true,
      rank: 0,
      score: rankingScore(s.company.size, pValue, pRep, pCond),
      size: s.company.size,
      reputation: pRep,
      value: pValue,
      condition: pCond,
      opinionOfPlayer: null,
      trend: {
        value: prev ? trendRatio(pValue, prev.value, TUNING.trendMinRatio) : 0,
        reputation: prev ? trendDiff(pRep, prev.reputation, TUNING.trendMinDelta) : 0,
        condition: pTrendCond,
      },
    },
  ];

  for (const c of s.competitors ?? []) {
    rows.push({
      id: c.id,
      name: c.name,
      isPlayer: false,
      rank: 0,
      score: rankingScore(c.size, c.value, c.reputation, c.condition),
      size: c.size,
      reputation: c.reputation,
      value: c.value,
      condition: c.condition,
      opinionOfPlayer: c.opinionOfPlayer,
      trend: c.trend,
    });
  }

  rows.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'pl'));
  rows.forEach((r, i) => {
    r.rank = i + 1;
  });
  return rows;
}

/** Statystyki gracza „tu i teraz” — zapisywane na początku tury do liczenia trendów. */
export function snapshotPlayer(s: GameState): PlayerSnapshot {
  const me = computeRanking(s).find((r) => r.isPlayer)!;
  return { value: me.value, reputation: me.reputation, condition: me.condition, rank: me.rank };
}

import type { GameState } from './types';
import {
  COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG,
  BELOW_REQUIRED_WORKERS_PENALTY,
  WORKER_CONFIG,
  SKARBOWKA_DECAY_BEZ_UMOWY,
  SKARBOWKA_DECAY_ZLECENIE,
  SKARBOWKA_GAIN_UOP,
  ACCOUNTANT_COST,
  ACCOUNTANT_BONUS
} from './constants';
import { makeRng } from './rng';

const BASE_REVENUE = 5000;
const CZYNSZ = 2500;
const TAX_RATE = 0.15;

// Nowa funkcja: Im więcej pracowników, tym mniejsza wydajność każdego kolejnego (min. 20%)
function calculateWorkerRevenue(count: number, baseRevenue: number): number {
  let total = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const efficiency = Math.max(0.2, 1 - (i * 0.05)); // 5% spadku za każdego kolejnego pracownika
    total += baseRevenue * efficiency;
  }
  return total;
}

export function tickEconomy(state: GameState): GameState {
  const oldProfitability = state.profitabilityScore;  
  const c = structuredClone(state.company);
  const f = c.finances;

  const baseRevenue = estimateRevenue(c);
  f.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci = baseRevenue;

  f.wydatki.pracownicy =
    c.employees.bezUmowy * WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.wage +
    c.employees.zlecenie * WORKER_CONFIG.zlecenie.wage +
    c.employees.uop      * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.wage;

  f.wydatki.czynsz = CZYNSZ;
  f.wydatki.podatki = Math.max(0, baseRevenue * TAX_RATE);

  f.wydatki.splacaneKredyty = 0;
  for (const loan of c.loans) {
    if (loan.remaining <= 0) continue;
    const installment = loan.remaining / Math.max(1, loan.quartersLeft);
    const interest = loan.remaining * loan.interestRate;
    f.wydatki.splacaneKredyty += installment + interest;
    loan.remaining -= installment;
    loan.quartersLeft -= 1;
  }

  // Logika Skarbówki
  let skarbowkaDelta =
    c.employees.bezUmowy * SKARBOWKA_DECAY_BEZ_UMOWY +
    c.employees.zlecenie * SKARBOWKA_DECAY_ZLECENIE +
    c.employees.uop * SKARBOWKA_GAIN_UOP;

  if (c.hasAccountant) {
    skarbowkaDelta += ACCOUNTANT_BONUS;
    f.wydatki.inne = ACCOUNTANT_COST; // Zamiast +=
  } else {
    f.wydatki.inne = 0; // Zerujemy, jeśli zwolniliśmy księgową
  }

  c.reputation.opiniaSkarbowa = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, c.reputation.opiniaSkarbowa + skarbowkaDelta));

  const inflow = sum(f.wplywy) - f.wplywy.srodkiZKredytow - f.wplywy.dofinansowaniaSzaraStrefa;
  const outflow = sum(f.wydatki);
  f.stanKonta += inflow - outflow;

  // Kara reputacyjna za za mało pracowników
  const totalWorkers = c.employees.bezUmowy + c.employees.zlecenie + c.employees.uop;
  const requiredWorkers = COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[c.size].requiredWorkers;
  if (totalWorkers < requiredWorkers) {
    c.reputation.opiniaBranza = Math.max(0, c.reputation.opiniaBranza - BELOW_REQUIRED_WORKERS_PENALTY);
  }

  // Efekty hipnozy
  if (c.flags?.includes('hipnoza_dziala')) {
    c.finances.stanKonta += 5000;
  }
  if (c.flags?.includes('hipnoza_nie_dziala')) {
    c.finances.stanKonta -= 5000;
  }

  state.company = c;

  if (f.stanKonta < 0) {
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = (state.bankruptWarningTurns ?? 0) + 1;
  } else {
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = 0;
  }

    // --- OBLICZANIE RENTOWNOŚCI ---
  // 1. Baza
  let score = 50;

  // 2. Wpływ rozmiaru firmy (większa firma = trudniej o rentowność)
  if (c.size === 'micro') score += 10;
  else if (c.size === 'small') score += 5;
  else if (c.size === 'medium') score -= 5;

  // 3. Wpływ wydajności pracowników (stosunek przychodu do kosztów pracowniczych)
  if (f.wydatki.pracownicy > 0) {
    const ratio = f.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci / f.wydatki.pracownicy;
    // Jeśli przychód z pracowników jest 2x większy niż ich pensje, dodajemy punkty
    score += Math.min(20, (ratio - 1) * 10);
  }

  // 4. Wpływ jakości/reputacji (opinia w branży i publiczna)
  const avgRep = (c.reputation.opiniaBranza + c.reputation.opiniaPubliczna) / 2;
  score += (avgRep - 50) / 2;

  // (Miejsce na przyszły popyt rynkowy: score += marketDemand;)

  // Klamrowanie 0-100
  state.profitabilityScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));

  // Ustalanie trendu na podstawie różnicy
  const diff = state.profitabilityScore - oldProfitability;
  if (diff > 5) state.profitabilityTrend = 'up2';
  else if (diff > 0) state.profitabilityTrend = 'up1';
  else if (diff < -5) state.profitabilityTrend = 'down2';
  else if (diff < 0) state.profitabilityTrend = 'down1';
  else state.profitabilityTrend = 'neutral';

    // Makroekonomia (wahania losowe)
  const macroRng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn + 555);
  state.macroStats.unemployment = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, state.macroStats.unemployment + (macroRng.next() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1)));
  state.macroStats.development = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, state.macroStats.development + (macroRng.next() > 0.6 ? 1 : 0)));

  return state;
}

function estimateRevenue(c: GameState['company']): number {
  // Używamy nowej funkcji zamiast prostego mnożenia
  const workerRevenue =
    calculateWorkerRevenue(c.employees.bezUmowy, WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.revenue) +
    calculateWorkerRevenue(c.employees.zlecenie, WORKER_CONFIG.zlecenie.revenue) +
    calculateWorkerRevenue(c.employees.uop, WORKER_CONFIG.uop.revenue);
    
  const activityMultiplier = 1 + (c.reputation.branza - 50) / 200;
  return (BASE_REVENUE + workerRevenue) * activityMultiplier;
}

function sum(obj: any): number {
  return Object.values(obj).reduce((a: number, b: any) => a + b, 0);
}

import type { GameState, GameEvent, EventConditions } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import eventsData from '../data/events.json';

const ALL_EVENTS: GameEvent[] = eventsData as GameEvent[];

export function matchesConditions(state: GameState, c: EventConditions): boolean {
  if (c.yearMin !== undefined && state.year < c.yearMin) return false;
  if (c.yearMax !== undefined && state.year > c.yearMax) return false;
  if (c.phase && !c.phase.includes(state.phase)) return false;
  if (c.difficulty && !c.difficulty.includes(state.difficulty)) return false;
  if (c.party && !c.party.includes(state.ceo.party)) return false;
  if (c.currentPartyId && (!state.ceo.currentPartyId || !c.currentPartyId.includes(state.ceo.currentPartyId))) return false;
  if (c.rulingParty && !c.rulingParty.some(p => state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(p))) return false;
  if (c.mafiaMember !== undefined && state.ceo.inMafia !== c.mafiaMember) return false;
  if (c.minStanKonta !== undefined && state.company.finances.stanKonta < c.minStanKonta) return false;
  if (c.maxStanKonta !== undefined && state.company.finances.stanKonta > c.maxStanKonta) return false;
  if (c.legalForm && !c.legalForm.includes(state.company.legalForm)) return false;
  if (c.activities && !c.activities.includes(state.company.activity)) return false;
  if (c.flags && !c.flags.every(f => state.ceo.flags.includes(f))) return false;
  if (c.notFlags && c.notFlags.some(f => state.ceo.flags.includes(f))) return false;
  if ((c as any).hasParty === true && !state.ceo.currentPartyId) return false;
  if ((c as any).hasParty === false && state.ceo.currentPartyId) return false;

  if (c.minReputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(c.minReputation)) {
      if ((state.company.reputation as any)[k] < (v as number)) return false;
    }
  }
  if (c.maxReputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(c.maxReputation)) {
      if ((state.company.reputation as any)[k] > (v as number)) return false;
    }
  }
  if (c.hasItem && !state.company.inventory.includes(c.hasItem)) return false;
  return true;
}

export function rollEvents(state: GameState): GameEvent[] {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn);
  const eligible = ALL_EVENTS.filter(e => {
    if (state.history.includes(e.id)) return false;
    return matchesConditions(state, e.conditions);
  });
  if (eligible.length === 0) return [];
  
  const picked = rng.weighted(eligible);
  return [picked];
}

// Logika płatności ratalnej
export function calculateInstallmentPrice(price: number, months: number): number {
  if (months <= 1) return price;
  // Im więcej rat, tym wyższe odsetki (np. 5% na miesiąc)
  const interest = 1 + (months * 0.05);
  return Math.round(price * interest);
}

export function createInitialState(opts: {

export function getEligibleActivities(state: GameState): string[] {
  const all = activitiesData as unknown as Activity[];
  const eligible = all.filter(a => matchesConditions(state, a.conditions));
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 7 + 3);
  return eligible
    .sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5)
    .slice(0, 3)
    .map(a => a.id);
}

function generatePartySchedule(seed: string): Record<string, { from: number; to: number }> {
  const rng = makeRng(seed, 12345);
  const schedule: Record<string, { from: number; to: number }> = {};
  for (const p of (partiesData as Party[])) {
    if (p.isPSL) {
      schedule[p.id] = { from: 1989, to: 9999 };
    } else {
      const fromShift = rng.int(-2, 2);
      const toShift = p.activeTo >= 9000 ? 0 : rng.int(-2, 2);
      schedule[p.id] = {
        from: p.activeFrom + fromShift,
        to: p.activeTo >= 9000 ? 9999 : p.activeTo + toShift,
      };
    }
  }
  return schedule;
}
