import type { GameState, NewsItem, GameEvent, Party } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import {
  RUMOR_TEMPLATES,
  MAX_NEWS,
  FAKE_RUMORS,
  MISLEADING_RUMOR_CHANCE,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_THRESHOLD,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_RETRY,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_FIRST,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_RETRY_CHANCE,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_EXPIRES_IN,
  SKARBOWKA_THRESHOLD,
  SKARBOWKA_PREPARE_TURNS,
  FLOWERS_COST,
  FLOWERS_BONUS,
} from './constants';
import { matchesConditions } from './events';
import { applyEffects } from './actions'; // <- upewnij się, że to jest stąd!
import eventsData from '../data/events.json';
import partiesData from '../data/parties.json';
import customNewsData from '../data/custom_news.json';
import { getPartySize } from './politics';

interface CustomNews {
  id: string;
  headline: string;
  body?: string;
  weight: number;
  conditions: GameEvent['conditions'];
}

export function addNewsItem(state: GameState, item: Omit<NewsItem, 'read'>): void {
  const fullItem: NewsItem = { ...item, read: false };
  state.news.unshift(fullItem);
  if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
}

export function generatePassiveNews(state: GameState): void {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 13 + 7);

  // 1. Plotki o nadchodzących eventach (mogą być prawdziwe lub fałszywe)
  const upcomingOnce = (eventsData as GameEvent[]).filter(e =>
    e.once &&
    !state.history.includes(e.id) &&
    e.conditions.yearMin !== undefined &&
    e.conditions.yearMin > state.year &&
    e.conditions.yearMin <= state.year + 2
  );

  if (rng.next() < 0.35) {
    const isFake = rng.next() < MISLEADING_RUMOR_CHANCE || upcomingOnce.length === 0;

    if (isFake) {
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `rumor_fake_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: rng.pick(FAKE_RUMORS),
        category: 'rumor',
      });
    } else {
      const ev = rng.pick(upcomingOnce);
      const template = rng.pick(RUMOR_TEMPLATES);
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `rumor_${state.turn}_${ev.id}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: template.replace('{topic}', ev.title),
        category: 'rumor',
      });
    }
  }

  // 2. Sondaże — co 4 tury w oknie 2 lat przed wyborami
  const electionIn = state.politics.nextParliamentaryElection - state.year;
  if (electionIn <= 2 && electionIn > 0 && state.turn % 4 === 0) {
    const active = activePartiesFor(state);
    if (active.length > 0) {
      const sorted = [...active].sort((a, b) => sizeRank(b, state.year) - sizeRank(a, state.year));
      const leader = sorted[0];
      const second = sorted[1];
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `poll_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: `Sondaż: ${leader.name} prowadzi, ${second?.name ?? 'drugie miejsce nieznane'} tuż za nim`,
        body: 'Badanie przeprowadzone na zlecenie lokalnej gazety.',
        category: 'poll',
      });
    }
  }

  // 3. Partie pojawiają się / znikają
  for (const [id, sched] of Object.entries(state.politics.partySchedule)) {
    const p = (partiesData as Party[]).find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!p) continue;

    if (sched.from === state.year && !state.history.includes(`appeared_${id}`)) {
      state.history.push(`appeared_${id}`);
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `party_appear_${id}_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: `Powstaje nowe ugrupowanie: ${p.name}`,
        body: `Grupa działaczy ogłasza utworzenie partii.`,
        category: 'party',
      });
    }
    if (sched.to === state.year && sched.to < 9999 && !state.history.includes(`disappeared_${id}`)) {
      state.history.push(`disappeared_${id}`);
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `party_disappear_${id}_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: `${p.name} rozwiązuje się`,
        body: `Partia nie zdołała utrzymać struktur.`,
        category: 'party',
      });
    }
  }

  // 4. Własne newsy (flavor)
  const eligibleCustom = (customNewsData as CustomNews[]).filter(c =>
    matchesConditions(state, c.conditions)
  );
  if (eligibleCustom.length > 0 && rng.next() < 0.4) {
    const picked = rng.weighted(eligibleCustom);
    addNewsItem(state, {
      id: `${picked.id}_${state.turn}`,
      turn: state.turn,
      year: state.year,
      quarter: state.quarter,
      headline: picked.headline,
      body: picked.body,
      category: 'custom',
    });
  }
}

export function activePartiesFor(state: GameState): Party[] {
  return (partiesData as Party[]).filter(p => {
    if (p.isPSL && !state.politics.pslAvailable) return false;
    const sched = state.politics.partySchedule[p.id];
    if (!sched) return false;
    return state.year >= sched.from && state.year <= sched.to;
  });
}

function sizeRank(p: Party, year: number): number {
  const size = getPartySize(p, year);
  return size === 'very_large' ? 5 : size === 'large' ? 4 : size === 'medium' ? 3 : size === 'small' ? 2 : 1;
}

export function addActionableNewsItem(
  state: GameState,
  item: Omit<NewsItem, 'read' | 'resolved'>
): void {
  const fullItem: NewsItem = {
    ...item,
    read: false,
    resolved: false,
  };
  state.news.unshift(fullItem);
  if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
}

export function processExpiredActions(state: GameState): void {
  for (const item of state.news) {
    if (item.resolved) continue;
    if (!item.choices) continue;
    if (item.expiresAtTurn === undefined) continue;
    if (state.turn < item.expiresAtTurn) continue;

    item.resolved = true;
    item.resolvedChoiceId = '__expired__';

    if (item.onExpireEffects) {
      applyEffects(state, item.onExpireEffects);
    }
  }
}

export function checkMafiaInvitation(state: GameState): void {
  // Warunki bazowe
  if (state.year < 1990) return;
  if (state.phase !== 'chaos') return;
  if (state.ceo.inMafia) return;
  if (state.ceo.flags.includes('zdradzil_mafie')) return;
  if (state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < MAFIA_INVITATION_THRESHOLD) return;

  // Sprawdź, czy w tej chwili nie ma już aktywnego zaproszenia w skrzynce
  const hasPending = state.news.some(
    n => n.id.startsWith('mafia_invitation_') && !n.resolved
  );
  if (hasPending) return;

  // Odstęp czasowy od poprzedniego zaproszenia
  const refusedBefore =
    state.ceo.flags.includes('mafia_refused') ||
    state.ceo.flags.includes('mafia_ghosted');

  const lastTurn = state.ceo.lastMafiaInvitationTurn ?? -999;
  const gap = state.turn - lastTurn;
  const minGap = refusedBefore ? MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_RETRY : MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_FIRST;
  if (gap < minGap) return;

  // Losowanie (przy ponownym zaproszeniu tylko 30% szansy na turę)
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 17 + 5);
  const chance = refusedBefore ? MAFIA_INVITATION_RETRY_CHANCE : 1.0;
  if (rng.next() > chance) return;

  // Wyślij zaproszenie
  const isRetry = refusedBefore;
  addActionableNewsItem(state, {
    id: `mafia_invitation_${state.turn}`,
    turn: state.turn,
    year: state.year,
    quarter: state.quarter,
    headline: isRetry ? 'Kolejna propozycja' : 'Propozycja nie do odrzucenia',
    body: isRetry
      ? 'Mężczyzna w drogim garniturze znowu się zjawił. Tym razem pyta wprost, czy zmieniłeś zdanie.'
      : 'Do twojego biura przyszedł mężczyzna w drogim garniturze. Bez zapowiedzi. Mówi, że reprezentuje "ludzi, którzy mogą wiele". Proponuje współpracę.',
    category: 'custom',
    expiresAtTurn: state.turn + MAFIA_INVITATION_EXPIRES_IN,
    choices: [
      {
        id: 'accept',
        label: 'Wchodzę w to',
        description: 'Akceptujesz propozycję. Od teraz masz nowych partnerów.',
        effects: {
          joinMafia: true,
          reputation: { opiniaMafia: 10, opiniaPubliczna: -5 },
          flagsAdd: ['w_mafii'],
        },
      },
      {
        id: 'refuse',
        label: 'Odmawiam',
        description: 'Nie chcesz mieć z tym nic wspólnego. Ryzykujesz konsekwencje.',
        effects: {
          reputation: { opiniaMafia: -25 },
          flagsAdd: ['mafia_refused'],
        },
      },
    ],
    onExpireEffects: {
      reputation: { opiniaMafia: -10 },
      flagsAdd: ['mafia_ghosted'],
    },
  });

  state.ceo.lastMafiaInvitationTurn = state.turn;
}

import type {
  GameState,
  ElectionResult,
  Party,
  Ideology,
  PresidentialCandidate,
  PresidentialResult,
  PartySize
} from './types';
import { COALITION_RULES, CANDIDATE_FIRST_NAMES, CANDIDATE_LAST_NAMES, PARTY_SIZE_WEIGHT, ELECTION_YEARS } from './constants';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import partiesData from '../data/parties.json';
import presidentialElectionsData from '../data/presidential_elections.json';

const SEATS_TOTAL = 460;

interface PredefinedCandidate {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  partyId: string | null;
  partyLabel?: string;
  minPercent: number;
  maxPercent: number;
  typicalPercent: number;
}

interface PredefinedElection {
  year: number;
  candidates: PredefinedCandidate[];
  secondRoundWinnerId?: string;
  secondRoundChance?: number;
}

export function tickPolitics(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (state.year >= state.politics.nextParliamentaryElection) {
    state.politics = runParliamentaryElection(state);
    state.lastElectionResult = state.politics.lastElection;

    const nextElectionYear = ELECTION_YEARS.find(y => y > state.year);
    state.politics.nextParliamentaryElection = nextElectionYear ?? (state.year + 4);
  }
  
  if (state.year >= state.politics.nextPresidentialElection) {
    const result = computePresidentialElection(state);
    state.lastPresidentialResult = result;
    state.politics = {
      ...state.politics,
      president: {
        id: result.winner.id,
        name: result.winner.name,
        partyId: result.winner.partyId,
        termStart: state.year,
        termEnd: state.year + 5,
      },
      nextPresidentialElection: state.politics.nextPresidentialElection + 5,
    };
  }
  return state;
}

export function getPartySize(party: Party, year: number): PartySize {
  const sched = party.sizeSchedule.find(s => year >= s.from && year <= s.to);
  return sched ? sched.size : 'medium';
}

function activeParties(state: GameState): Party[] {
  return (partiesData as Party[]).filter(p => {
    if (p.isPSL && !state.politics.pslAvailable) return false;
    const sched = state.politics.partySchedule[p.id];
    const isYearValid = sched ? (state.year >= sched.from && state.year <= sched.to) : (state.year >= p.activeFrom && state.year <= p.activeTo);
    if (!isYearValid) return false;

    if (p.pzprAvailable !== 'both') {
      const playerIsPzpr = state.ceo.party === 'PZPR';
      if (playerIsPzpr && p.pzprAvailable === 'no') return false;
      if (!playerIsPzpr && p.pzprAvailable === 'yes') return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}

function randomName(rng: ReturnType<typeof makeRng>): string {
  return `${rng.pick(CANDIDATE_FIRST_NAMES)} ${rng.pick(CANDIDATE_LAST_NAMES)}`;
}

function computePresidentialElection(state: GameState): PresidentialResult {
  const predefined = (presidentialElectionsData as PredefinedElection[]).find(e => e.year === state.year);
  if (predefined) return computeFromPredefined(state, predefined);
  return computeRandomElection(state);
}

function computeFromPredefined(state: GameState, pre: PredefinedElection): PresidentialResult {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 100 + 2);

  const candidates: PresidentialCandidate[] = pre.candidates.map(c => {
    const mid = (c.minPercent + c.maxPercent) / 2;
    const pull = c.typicalPercent - mid;
    const rolls = (rng.next() + rng.next() + rng.next()) / 3;
    const base = c.minPercent + (c.maxPercent - c.minPercent) * rolls;
    const value = Math.max(c.minPercent, Math.min(c.maxPercent, base + pull));

    return {
      id: c.id,
      name: c.name,
      partyId: c.partyId,
      partyLabel: c.partyLabel,
      votesPercent: value,
    };
  });

  const total = candidates.reduce((s, c) => s + c.votesPercent, 0);
  for (const c of candidates) c.votesPercent = (c.votesPercent / total) * 100;
  candidates.sort((a, b) => b.votesPercent - a.votesPercent);

  let winner = candidates[0];
  let secondRound = false;
  let secondRoundCandidates: PresidentialCandidate[] | undefined;

  if (candidates.length > 1 && candidates[0].votesPercent < 50) {
    secondRound = true;
    const c1 = candidates[0];
    const c2 = candidates[1];
    secondRoundCandidates = [c1, c2];

    if (pre.secondRoundWinnerId) {
      const forced = candidates.find(c => c.id === pre.secondRoundWinnerId);
      if (forced && (forced.id === c1.id || forced.id === c2.id)) {
        const chance = pre.secondRoundChance ?? 1.0;
        winner = rng.next() < chance ? forced : (forced.id === c1.id ? c2 : c1);
      }
    } else {
      const diff = (c1.votesPercent - c2.votesPercent) / 100;
      const c1Chance = Math.min(0.85, Math.max(0.5, 0.5 + diff + 0.1));
      winner = rng.next() < c1Chance ? c1 : c2;
    }
  }

  return { year: state.year, candidates, winner, secondRound, secondRoundCandidates };
}

function computeRandomElection(state: GameState): PresidentialResult {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 100 + 2);
  const parties = activeParties(state);
  const candidates: PresidentialCandidate[] = [];

  for (const p of parties) {
    const size = getPartySize(p, state.year);
    const base = PARTY_SIZE_WEIGHT[size] || 0.1; // Pobiera wagę zależną od roku
    const noise = (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.08;
    const strength = Math.max(0.02, base + noise);
    candidates.push({
      id: `cand_${p.id}_${state.year}`,
      name: randomName(rng),
      partyId: p.id,
      votesPercent: strength * 100,
    });
  }

  const indepCount = rng.next() < 0.5 ? 1 : 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < indepCount; i++) {
    candidates.push({
      id: `cand_indep_${state.year}_${i}`,
      name: randomName(rng),
      partyId: null,
      votesPercent: (0.05 + rng.next() * 0.10) * 100,
    });
  }

  const total = candidates.reduce((s, c) => s + c.votesPercent, 0);
  for (const c of candidates) c.votesPercent = (c.votesPercent / total) * 100;
  candidates.sort((a, b) => b.votesPercent - a.votesPercent);

  let winner = candidates[0];
  let secondRound = false;
  let secondRoundCandidates: PresidentialCandidate[] | undefined;

  if (candidates.length > 1 && candidates[0].votesPercent < 50) {
    secondRound = true;
    const c1 = candidates[0];
    const c2 = candidates[1];
    secondRoundCandidates = [c1, c2];
    const diff = (c1.votesPercent - c2.votesPercent) / 100;
    const c1Chance = Math.min(0.85, Math.max(0.5, 0.5 + diff + 0.1));
    winner = rng.next() < c1Chance ? c1 : c2;
  }

  return { year: state.year, candidates, winner, secondRound, secondRoundCandidates };
}

function runParliamentaryElection(state: GameState) {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 100 + 1);
  const parties = activeParties(state);
  const seats: Record<string, number> = {};
  let remaining = SEATS_TOTAL;

  for (const p of parties) {
    const size = getPartySize(p, state.year);
    const base = PARTY_SIZE_WEIGHT[size] || 0.1; // Pobiera wagę zależną od roku
    const noise = (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.08;
    const share = Math.max(0, base + noise);
    const s = Math.floor(share * SEATS_TOTAL);
    seats[p.id] = s;
    remaining -= s;
  }
  
  if (parties.length > 0) {
    const largest = parties.reduce((a, b) => (seats[a.id] > seats[b.id] ? a : b));
    seats[largest.id] += remaining;
  }

  const coalition = formCoalition(parties, seats);
  const premier = coalition[0] ?? null;

  const result: ElectionResult = { year: state.year, seats, coalition, premierId: premier };
  
  return {
    ...state.politics,
    parties,
    lastElection: result,
    rulingCoalition: coalition,
    premierId: premier,
  };
}

function formCoalition(parties: Party[], seats: Record<string, number>): string[] {
  const sorted = [...parties].sort((a, b) => seats[b.id] - seats[a.id]);
  const coalition: string[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  for (const p of sorted) {
    if (total >= SEATS_TOTAL / 2 + 1) break;
    if (coalition.length === 0) {
      coalition.push(p.id);
      total += seats[p.id];
      continue;
    }
    const canJoin = coalition.some(memberId => {
      const member = parties.find(x => x.id === memberId)!;
      return canCoalition(member.ideology, p.ideology);
    });
    if (canJoin) {
      coalition.push(p.id);
      total += seats[p.id];
    }
  }
  return coalition;
}

export function canCoalition(a: Ideology, b: Ideology): boolean {
  if (a === b) return true;
  return COALITION_RULES[a].includes(b) || COALITION_RULES[b].includes(a);
}

import type { GameState, Reputation } from './types';
import { REPUTATION_WEIGHTS, MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD, PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS } from './constants';

const clamp = (x: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, x));

export function politicianWeight(state: GameState): number {
  const ourParty = state.ceo.currentPartyId;
  if (!ourParty) return 0.2;
  const inCoalition = state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(ourParty);
  if (inCoalition) {
    const isPremier = state.politics.premierId === ourParty;
    const isPresident = state.politics.president?.partyId === ourParty;
    if (isPremier && isPresident) return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.bothPresidentAndPremier;
    if (isPresident) return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.onlyPresident;
    if (isPremier) return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.onlyPremier;
  }
  return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.opposition;
}

export function recomputeReputation(state: GameState): Reputation {
  const r = { ...state.company.reputation };

  r.branza = clamp(r.branza);
  r.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(r.opiniaPubliczna);
  r.opiniaPracownicy = clamp(r.opiniaPracownicy);
  r.opiniaBranza = clamp(r.opiniaBranza);
  r.opiniaMafia = clamp(r.opiniaMafia);
  r.opiniaMedia = clamp(r.opiniaMedia);
  r.opiniaPolitycy = clamp(r.opiniaPolitycy);
  r.opiniaSkarbowa = clamp(r.opiniaSkarbowa);

  const mediaInf = (r.opiniaMedia - 50) * 0.1;
  const polWeight = politicianWeight(state);
  const polInf = (r.opiniaPolitycy - 50) * polWeight * 0.15;

  r.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(r.opiniaPubliczna + mediaInf + polInf);
  r.opiniaBranza    = clamp(r.opiniaBranza    + mediaInf * 0.5 + polInf * 0.5);
  r.opiniaMafia     = clamp(r.opiniaMafia     + mediaInf * 0.3 + polInf * 0.3);

  if (r.opiniaMafia > MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD) {
    const overshoot = r.opiniaMafia - MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD;
    r.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(r.opiniaPubliczna - overshoot * 0.3);
  }

  let sum = 0;
  for (const [key, weight] of Object.entries(REPUTATION_WEIGHTS)) {
    sum += (r as any)[key] * weight;
  }
  r.ogolna = clamp(sum);

  return r;
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\engine\rng.ts ===
import seedrandom from 'seedrandom';

export function makeRng(seed: string, salt: number) {
  const rng = seedrandom(`${seed}:${salt}`);
  return {
    next: () => rng(),
    int: (min: number, max: number) => Math.floor(rng() * (max - min + 1)) + min,
    pick: <T>(arr: T[]) => arr[Math.floor(rng() * arr.length)],
    weighted: <T extends { weight: number }>(arr: T[]) => {
      const total = arr.reduce((s, x) => s + x.weight, 0);
      let r = rng() * total;
      for (const item of arr) {
        r -= item.weight;
        if (r <= 0) return item;
      }
      return arr[arr.length - 1];
    },
  };
}

import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';

function allActivityIds(): string[] {
  const raw: any = branchesData;
  const cats: any[] = Array.isArray(raw)
    ? raw
    : Array.isArray(raw?.categories)
      ? raw.categories
      : Object.values(raw ?? {});
  const ids: string[] = [];
  for (const cat of cats) for (const a of cat?.activities ?? []) if (a?.id) ids.push(a.id);
  return ids;
}
 
const ACTIVITY_IDS = allActivityIds();
 
function newGame(seed: string, i: number): GameState {
  const s = createInitialState({
    seed,
    difficulty: (['easy', 'medium', 'hard'] as const)[i % 3],
    inPZPR: i % 2 === 0,
    activity: ACTIVITY_IDS[i % ACTIVITY_IDS.length],
    companyName: 'Test',
    suffix: 'sp. z o.o.',
    size: 'micro',
  });
  s.currentActivities = getEligibleActivities(s);
  return s;
}
 
 
const median = (a: number[]) => {
  const b = [...a].sort((x, y) => x - y);
  return b.length ? b[Math.floor(b.length / 2)] : 0;
};
 
function findBadNumbers(obj: unknown, path = 'state', out: string[] = []): string[] {
  if (out.length > 5) return out;
  if (typeof obj === 'number') {
    if (!Number.isFinite(obj)) out.push(path);
  } else if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    obj.forEach((v, i) => findBadNumbers(v, `${path}[${i}]`, out));
  } else if (obj && typeof obj === 'object') {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) findBadNumbers(v, `${path}.${k}`, out);
  }
  return out;
}
 
function checkInvariants(s: GameState, before?: GameState): string[] {
  const v: string[] = [];
 
  const bad = findBadNumbers(s);
  if (bad.length) v.push(`NaN/Infinity w stanie: ${bad.slice(0, 3).join(', ')}`);
 
  for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(s.company.reputation)) {
    if (typeof val === 'number' && (val < -1e-9 || val > 100 + 1e-9)) v.push(`Reputacja poza 0-100: ${k}`);
  }
  for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(s.company.employees)) {
    if (val < 0 || !Number.isInteger(val)) v.push(`Zła liczba pracowników: ${k}`);
  }
  for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(s.company.resources)) {
    if (val < 0) v.push(`Ujemny zasób w magazynie: ${k}`);
  }
  for (const l of s.company.loans) {
    if (l.remaining < -1e-6) v.push('Ujemne saldo kredytu');
  }
  if (![1, 2, 3, 4].includes(s.quarter)) v.push(`Zły kwartał: ${s.quarter}`);
 
  const expectedPhase = s.year < 2005 ? 'chaos' : s.year < 2030 ? 'duopol' : 'invasion';
  if (s.phase !== expectedPhase) v.push('Faza nie pasuje do roku');
  if (s.pendingEvents.length > 1) v.push('Więcej niż 1 event naraz');
 
  if (before && !s.gameOver) {
    if (s.turn !== before.turn + 1) v.push('Licznik tury nie wzrósł o 1');
    const step = before.pace === 'year' ? 4 : 1;
    const dq = s.year * 4 + s.quarter - (before.year * 4 + before.quarter);
    if (dq !== step) v.push(`Data przesunęła się o ${dq} kwartałów zamiast ${step}`);
  }
  return v;
}
 
const CONTRACTS: ContractType[] = ['bezUmowy', 'zlecenie', 'uop'];
 
function randomActions(s: GameState, rng: Rng): GameState {
  const cash = s.company.finances.stanKonta;
  if (cash < 0 && s.bankruptWarningTurns > 0 && rng.next() < 0.7) return applyEmergencyLoan(s);
 
  const r = rng.next();
  if (r < 0.1) return applyHire(s, rng.pick(CONTRACTS), rng.pick([-1, 1, 1, 2]));
  if (r < 0.16 && cash < 50_000) {
    return applyLoan(s, rng.pick<'bank' | 'szara_strefa'>(['bank', 'szara_strefa']), rng.pick([10_000, 50_000, 100_000]));
  }
  if (r < 0.22) return buyAd(s, rng.pick<'radio' | 'tv' | 'internet'>(['radio', 'tv', 'internet']));
  if (r < 0.26) return changeCompanySize(s, rng.pick<'up' | 'down'>(['up', 'down']));
  if (r < 0.3) return buildBranch(s);
  if (r < 0.34) return rentWarehouse(s);
  if (r < 0.37) return toggleAccountant(s);
  if (r < 0.42 && s.politics.parties.length > 0) return applyJoinParty(s, rng.pick(s.politics.parties).id);
  if (r < 0.44) return applyLeaveParty(s);
  if (r < 0.46) return applySetPace(s, rng.pick<'quarter' | 'year'>(['quarter', 'year']));
  return s;
}
 
function playTurn(s: GameState, policy: Policy, rng: Rng): GameState {
  if (s.pendingEvents.length > 0) {
    const ev = s.pendingEvents[0];
    const choices = ev.choices.filter((c) => !c.requires || matchesConditions(s, c.requires));
    if (choices.length > 0) {
      const choice = policy === 'passive' ? choices[0] : rng.pick(choices);
      s = resolveEventChoice(s, ev.id, choice.id);
    }
  }
  if (s.gameOver) return s;
 
  if (s.currentActivities.length > 0 && (policy === 'passive' || rng.next() < 0.8)) {
    const id = policy === 'passive' ? s.currentActivities[0] : rng.pick(s.currentActivities);
    s = consumeActivity(s, id);
  }
  if (s.gameOver || policy === 'passive') return s;
 
  return randomActions(s, rng);
}
 
interface GameReport {
  final: GameState;
  violations: string[];
  eventTurns: number;
  emptyTurns: number;
  chases: number;
  combats: number;
  maxCash: number;
}
 
function playGame(seed: string, i: number, policy: Policy): GameReport {
  const mathRng = makeRng(seed, 777);
  vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockImplementation(() => mathRng.next());
 
  const rng = makeRng(seed, 1_000_001);
  let s = newGame(seed, i);
  const rep: GameReport = { final: s, violations: [], eventTurns: 0, emptyTurns: 0, chases: 0, combats: 0, maxCash: 0 };
 
  const check = (label: string, before?: GameState) => {
    for (const msg of checkInvariants(s, before)) rep.violations.push(`${msg}  [${seed}, tura ${s.turn}, ${label}]`);
  };
 
  for (let step = 0; step < 400 && !s.gameOver && s.year < 2031; step++) {
    s = playTurn(s, policy, rng);
    check('akcje gracza');
    if (s.gameOver) break;
 
    const before = s;
    s = nextTurn(s);
    check('nextTurn', before);
 
    if (s.pendingEvents.length > 0) rep.eventTurns++;
    else if (s.year < 2030) rep.emptyTurns++;
    if (s.activeChase) { rep.chases++; s.activeChase = null; }
    if (s.activeCombat) { rep.combats++; s.activeCombat = null; }
    rep.maxCash = Math.max(rep.maxCash, s.company.finances.stanKonta);
  }
 
  if (s.gameOver && nextTurn(s) !== s) rep.violations.push(`nextTurn działa po gameOver  [${seed}]`);
  rep.final = s;
  return rep;
}

describe('symulacja headless', () => {

const REPUTATION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  branza: 'Branża',
  opiniaPubliczna: 'Opinia publiczna',
  opiniaPracownicy: 'Opinia pracowników',
  opiniaBranza: 'Opinia w branży',

  zlecenie: 'zlecenie',

const signed = (v: number) => `${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v.toLocaleString('pl-PL')}`;

function describeEffects(e: EventEffects): string[] {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  if (e.stanKonta) lines.push(`Stan konta: ${signed(e.stanKonta)} PLN`);
  if (e.reputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.reputation)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`${REPUTATION_LABELS[k] ?? k}: ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.wydatki) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wydatki)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`Wydatki (${k}): ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.wplywy) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wplywy)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`Wpływy (${k}): ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.employeesDelta) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.employeesDelta)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`Pracownicy (${CONTRACT_LABELS[k] ?? k}): ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.divideFinances) lines.push('Podział finansów');
  if (e.addItem) lines.push(`Nowy przedmiot: ${e.addItem}`);
  if (e.setParty) lines.push(`Zmiana strony politycznej: ${e.setParty}`);
  if (e.joinMafia) lines.push('Dołączasz do mafii');
  if (e.leaveMafia) lines.push('Opuszczasz mafię');
  if (e.setCurrentPartyId) lines.push(`Wstępujesz do partii: ${e.setCurrentPartyId}`);
  if (e.flagsAdd?.length) lines.push(`Nowe znaczniki: ${e.flagsAdd.join(', ')}`);
  if (e.flagsRemove?.length) lines.push(`Usunięte znaczniki: ${e.flagsRemove.join(', ')}`);
  if (lines.length === 0) lines.push('Brak widocznych skutków.');
  return lines;
}

export function nextTurn(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (state.gameOver) return state;

  let s = structuredClone(state);
  const rng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn);
  const quarters = s.pace === 'year' ? 4 : 1;
  const oldPhase = s.phase;

  // 1. AKTUALIZACJA CZASU (przed ekonomią i eventami!)
  s.turn += 1;
  const totalQuarters = s.year * 4 + (s.quarter - 1) + quarters;
  s.year = Math.floor(totalQuarters / 4);
  s.quarter = ((totalQuarters % 4) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
  s.phase = s.year < 2005 ? 'chaos' : s.year < 2030 ? 'duopol' : 'invasion';

  if (s.phase !== oldPhase) {
    s.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[s.phase] = 0;
  }

  // 2. EKONOMIA I ROZLICZENIA
  for (let i = 0; i < quarters; i++) {
    resolvePendingSales(s);
    s = tickEconomy(s);
  }

  // 3. POLITYKA I EVENTY
  s = tickPolitics(s);
  s.pendingEvents = rollEvents(s);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  if (s.bankruptWarningTurns >= BANKRUPT_GRACE_TURNS && s.company.finances.stanKonta < 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Bankructwo — nie spłaciłeś długów' };
  }
  if (s.company.reputation.ogolna <= 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Utrata reputacji' };
  }
  if (s.gameOver) return s;

  // 4. AKTYWNOŚCI I POWIADOMIENIA
  s.currentActivities = rollActivities(s, rng);
  s.chosenActivityThisTurn = null;
  s.lastChoiceResult = null;

  generatePassiveNews(s);
  checkMafiaInvitation(s);
  checkTaxOfficeNotice(s);
  processExpiredActions(s);
  checkEmployeeUnrest(s);

  if (s.skarbowkaVisitTurn !== null && s.turn === s.skarbowkaVisitTurn && !s.activeCombat) {
    startTaxCombat(s);
  }

  if (!s.activeChase && !s.activeCombat && s.ceo.inMafia === false && s.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < CHASE_MAFIA_THRESHOLD) {
    const chaseRng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn * 99 + 1);
    if (chaseRng.next() < CHASE_CHANCE) {
      startChase(s);
    }
  }
  
  return s;
}

function rollActivities(state: GameState, rng: ReturnType<typeof makeRng>): string[] {
  const pool = (activitiesData as Activity[])
    .filter((a) => matchesConditions(state, a.conditions))
    .map((a) => ({ id: a.id, weight: a.weight > 0 ? a.weight : 1 }));

  const picked: string[] = [];
  while (picked.length < ACTIVITIES_PER_TURN && pool.length > 0) {
    const item = rng.weighted(pool);
    picked.push(item.id);
    pool.splice(pool.findIndex((p) => p.id === item.id), 1);
  }
  return picked;
}

export function consumeActivity(state: GameState, activityId: string): GameState {
  if (state.gameOver) return state;
  if (state.chosenActivityThisTurn) return state;
  if (!state.currentActivities.includes(activityId)) return state;

  const activity = (activitiesData as Activity[]).find((a) => a.id === activityId);
  if (!activity) return state;

  const s = structuredClone(state);
  applyEffects(s, activity.effects);
  s.chosenActivityThisTurn = activityId;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  if (s.company.reputation.ogolna <= 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Utrata reputacji' };
  }
  return s;
}

export function resolveEventChoice(state: GameState, eventId: string, choiceId: string): GameState {
  const ev = state.pendingEvents.find((e) => e.id === eventId);
  const choice = ev?.choices.find((c) => c.id === choiceId);
  if (!ev || !choice) return state;

  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const rng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn * 31 + s.history.length);

  let gambleResult: 'win' | 'lose' | undefined;
  let effects: EventEffects | undefined;
  let outcomeDescription: string | undefined;

  if (choice.outcomes && choice.outcomes.length > 0) {
    const picked = rng.weighted(choice.outcomes);
    effects = picked.effects;
    outcomeDescription = picked.description;
  } else if (choice.gamble) {
    if (rng.next() < choice.gamble.winChance) {
      effects = choice.gamble.onWin;
      gambleResult = 'win';
      outcomeDescription = choice.gamble.winText;
    } else {
      effects = choice.gamble.onLose;
      gambleResult = 'lose';
      outcomeDescription = choice.gamble.loseText;
    }
  } else {
    effects = choice.effects;
  }
  if (effects) applyEffects(s, effects);

  const result: ChoiceResult = {
    eventId: ev.id,
    eventTitle: ev.title,
    choiceId: choice.id,
    choiceLabel: choice.label,
    consequenceLines: effects ? describeEffects(effects) : ['Brak efektów.'],
    gambleResult,
    outcomeDescription,
  };

  s.pendingEvents = [];
  s.history.push(eventId);
  s.lastChoiceResult = result;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < INSTANT_BANKRUPTCY_LIMIT) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Bankructwo — nie spłaciłeś długów' };
  }
  if (s.company.reputation.ogolna <= 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Utrata reputacji' };
  }

  return s;
}

export function dismissConsequence(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  s.lastChoiceResult = null;
  return s;
}

import { movePlayer, dismissChase as dismissChaseEngine } from '../../engine/chase';
import { autosave } from '../autosave';

export interface ChaseSlice {
  chaseMove: (dx: number, dy: number) => void;
  dismissChase: () => void;
}

export const createChaseSlice: StateCreator<any, [], [], ChaseSlice> = (set, get) => ({
  chaseMove: (dx, dy) => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s || !s.activeChase) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    movePlayer(next, dx, dy);
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },
  dismissChase: () => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    dismissChaseEngine(next);
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },
});

import type { StateCreator } from 'zustand';
import type { ContractType, GameState, Pace } from '../../engine/types';
import {
  nextTurn,
  consumeActivity as consumeActivityEngine,
  resolveEventChoice,
  dismissConsequence,
} from '../../engine/turn';
import {
  applyHire,
  applyLoan,
  applyChangeActivity,
  applyJoinParty,
  applyLeaveParty,
  applyEmergencyLoan,
  applySetPace,
  toggleAccountant,
  changeCompanySize as changeCompanySizeEngine,
  buildBranch as buildBranchEngine,
  buyResource as buyResourceEngine,
  sellResource as sellResourceEngine,
  buyAd as buyAdEngine,
  buyGoods as buyGoodsEngine,
  sellGoods as sellGoodsEngine,
  resolveComplaint as resolveComplaintEngine,
  rentWarehouse as rentWarehouseEngine,
  interactWithCompetitor as interactWithCompetitorEngine,
} from '../../engine/actions';
import { autosave } from '../autosave';

// Typy argumentów handlu bierzemy prosto z silnika, zamiast `any`.
type Supplier = Parameters<typeof buyGoodsEngine>[1];
type Buyer = Parameters<typeof sellGoodsEngine>[1];

export interface GameSlice {
  doTurn: () => void;
  consumeActivity: (activityId: string) => void;
  resolveEvent: (eventId: string, choiceId: string) => void;
  dismissConsequence: () => void;
  joinParty: (partyId: string) => void;
  leaveParty: () => void;
  changeActivity: (activity: string) => void;
  takeEmergencyLoan: () => void;
  hireWorker: (contract: ContractType, delta: number) => void;
  takeLoan: (source: 'bank' | 'szara_strefa', amount: number) => void;
  setPace: (pace: Pace) => void;
  toggleAccountant: () => void;
  dismissElection: () => void;
  dismissPresidentialResult: () => void;
  changeCompanySize: (direction: 'up' | 'down') => void;
  buildBranch: () => void;
  buyResource: (resId: string, amount: number, basePrice: number) => void;
  sellResource: (resId: string, amount: number, basePrice: number) => void;
  buyAd: (type: 'radio' | 'tv' | 'internet') => void;
  buyGoods: (supplier: Supplier, amount: number) => void;
  sellGoods: (buyer: Buyer, resId: string, amount: number, baseValue: number) => void;
  resolveComplaint: (id: string, accept: boolean) => void;
  rentWarehouse: () => void;
  interactCompetitor: (compId: string, type: 'positive' | 'negative') => void;
}

interface CommitOptions {
  /** Akcje „zamykające okienka” mają działać także po końcu gry. */
  allowAfterGameOver?: boolean;
}

export const createGameSlice: StateCreator<any, [], [], GameSlice> = (set, get) => {
  /**
   * Jedyne miejsce, które: pobiera stan, blokuje akcje po końcu gry,
   * odpala funkcję silnika, zapisuje stan w store i robi autosave.
   */
  const commit = (fn: (s: GameState) => GameState, opts: CommitOptions = {}) => {
    const s: GameState | null = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    if (s.gameOver && !opts.allowAfterGameOver) return;

    const next = fn(s);
    if (next === s) return; // nic się nie zmieniło — bez zbędnego zapisu

    // Najpierw stan, potem zapis: błąd localStorage (np. pełny) nie może zgubić akcji gracza.
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    try {
      autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[autosave] zapis nie powiódł się:', err);
    }
  };

  return {
    // --- Tura ---
    doTurn: () => commit(nextTurn),
    consumeActivity: (id) => commit((s) => consumeActivityEngine(s, id)),
    resolveEvent: (eventId, choiceId) => commit((s) => resolveEventChoice(s, eventId, choiceId)),
    dismissConsequence: () => commit(dismissConsequence, { allowAfterGameOver: true }),

    // --- Polityka ---
    joinParty: (partyId) => commit((s) => applyJoinParty(s, partyId)),
    leaveParty: () => commit(applyLeaveParty),
    dismissElection: () =>
      commit((s) => ({ ...s, lastElectionResult: null }), { allowAfterGameOver: true }),
    dismissPresidentialResult: () =>
      commit((s) => ({ ...s, lastPresidentialResult: null }), { allowAfterGameOver: true }),

    // --- Firma ---
    changeActivity: (activity) => commit((s) => applyChangeActivity(s, activity)),
    hireWorker: (contract, delta) => commit((s) => applyHire(s, contract, delta)),
    setPace: (pace) => commit((s) => applySetPace(s, pace)),
    toggleAccountant: () => commit(toggleAccountant),
    changeCompanySize: (direction) => commit((s) => changeCompanySizeEngine(s, direction)),
    buildBranch: () => commit(buildBranchEngine),
    buyAd: (type) => commit((s) => buyAdEngine(s, type)),

    // --- Finanse ---
    takeEmergencyLoan: () => commit(applyEmergencyLoan),
    takeLoan: (source, amount) => commit((s) => applyLoan(s, source, amount)),

    // --- Handel ---
    buyResource: (resId, amount, price) => commit((s) => buyResourceEngine(s, resId, amount, price)),
    sellResource: (resId, amount, price) => commit((s) => sellResourceEngine(s, resId, amount, price)),
    buyGoods: (supplier, amount) => commit((s) => buyGoodsEngine(s, supplier, amount)),
    sellGoods: (buyer, resId, amount, baseValue) =>
      commit((s) => sellGoodsEngine(s, buyer, resId, amount, baseValue)),
    resolveComplaint: (id, accept) => commit((s) => resolveComplaintEngine(s, id, accept)),
    rentWarehouse: () => commit(rentWarehouseEngine),
    interactCompetitor: (compId, type) => {
      const s = get().state;
      if (!s) return;
      const next = interactWithCompetitorEngine(s, compId, type);
      autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
      set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    }

  };

  
};

import type { StateCreator } from 'zustand';
import { applyEffects } from '../../engine/actions';
import { recomputeReputation } from '../../engine/reputation';
import { autosave } from '../autosave';
import type { NewsItem } from '../../engine/types';

export interface NewsSlice {
  markNewsRead: (id: string) => void;
  resolveNewsChoice: (newsId: string, choiceId: string) => void;
  devAddTestNews: () => void;
}

export const createNewsSlice: StateCreator<any, [], [], NewsSlice> = (set, get) => ({
  markNewsRead: (id) => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = { ...s, news: s.news.map((n: NewsItem) => n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n) };
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },
  resolveNewsChoice: (newsId, choiceId) => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    const item = next.news.find((n: NewsItem) => n.id === newsId);
    if (!item || item.resolved || !item.choices) return;
    const choice = item.choices.find((c: any) => c.id === choiceId);
    if (!choice) return;

    if (choice.effects) applyEffects(next, choice.effects);
    item.resolved = true;
    item.resolvedChoiceId = choiceId;
    item.read = true;
    next.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(next);
    
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },
  devAddTestNews: () => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    next.news.unshift({
      id: `test_action_${next.turn}_${next.news.length}`,
      turn: next.turn,
      year: next.year,
      quarter: next.quarter,
      headline: 'Testowa wiadomość z akcją',
      body: 'To jest test systemu ActionableNews.\nWybierz jedną z opcji poniżej.',
      category: 'custom',
      read: false,
      resolved: false,
      expiresAtTurn: next.turn + 3,
      choices: [
        { id: 'a', label: 'Opcja A — dostajesz 10 000 PLN', effects: { stanKonta: 10000 } },
        { id: 'b', label: 'Opcja B — tracisz 5 000 PLN', effects: { stanKonta: -5000 } },
      ],
      onExpireEffects: { reputation: { opiniaPubliczna: -3 } },
    });
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },
});
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\store\autosave.ts ===
import type { GameState } from '../engine/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from './settingsStore';

const SAVES_KEY = 'pp-saves-v1';

export interface SaveSlot {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: number;
  lastPlayed: number;
  state: GameState;
}

export type SaveSlots = Record<string, SaveSlot>;

export function loadSlotsFromStorage(): SaveSlots {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVES_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function persistSlots(slots: SaveSlots) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SAVES_KEY, JSON.stringify(slots));
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

export function autosave(state: GameState, slotId: string | null) {
  if (!useSettingsStore.getState().autosave || !slotId) return;
  const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
  const existing = slots[slotId];
  slots[slotId] = {
    id: slotId,
    name: existing?.name ?? (state.company.name + state.company.suffix),
    createdAt: existing?.createdAt ?? Date.now(),
    lastPlayed: Date.now(),
    state,
  };
  persistSlots(slots);
}

import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { GameState, Difficulty, CompanySize, AppScreen } from '../engine/types';
import { createInitialState, getEligibleActivities } from '../engine/initialState';
import { useSettingsStore } from './settingsStore';
import { loadSlotsFromStorage, persistSlots } from './autosave';
import type { SaveSlots } from './autosave';
import type { CombatSlice } from './slices/combatSlice';
import { createCombatSlice } from './slices/combatSlice';
import type { NewsSlice } from './slices/newsSlice';
import { createNewsSlice } from './slices/newsSlice';
import type { GameSlice } from './slices/gameSlice';
import { createGameSlice } from './slices/gameSlice';
import type { ChaseSlice } from './slices/chaseSlice';
import { createChaseSlice } from './slices/chaseSlice';

export const MAX_SLOTS = 5;
export const SLOT_IDS = ['slot_1', 'slot_2', 'slot_3', 'slot_4', 'slot_5'] as const;

interface NewGameOpts { 
  difficulty: Difficulty; 
  inPZPR: boolean; 
  activity: string; 
  companyName: string; 
  suffix: string; 
  size: CompanySize; 
}

interface GameStore extends CombatSlice, NewsSlice, GameSlice, ChaseSlice {
  // interactWithCompetitor: any; // Jeśli nie używasz tej wersji z "With", możesz to zostawić usunięte
  
  state: GameState | null;
  currentSlotId: string | null;
  screen: AppScreen;
  savesMode: 'new' | 'load';
  pendingSlotId: string | null;
  savesVersion: number;

  setScreen: (s: AppScreen) => void;
  openSaves: (mode: 'new' | 'load') => void;
  setPendingSlotId: (id: string | null) => void;
  
  newGame: (opts: NewGameOpts, slotId: string) => void;
  saveGame: () => void;
  loadGame: (slotId: string) => boolean;
  deleteSave: (slotId: string) => void;
  getSlots: () => SaveSlots;
  hasAnySave: () => boolean;
}

export const useGameStore = create<GameStore>()((set, get, store) => ({
  ...createCombatSlice(set, get, store),
  ...createNewsSlice(set, get, store),
  ...createGameSlice(set, get, store),
  ...createChaseSlice(set, get, store),

  state: null,
  currentSlotId: null,
  screen: 'menu' as AppScreen,
  savesMode: 'load' as 'new' | 'load',
  pendingSlotId: null,
  savesVersion: 0,

  setScreen: (s) => set({ screen: s }),
  openSaves: (mode) => set({ screen: 'saves', savesMode: mode }),
  setPendingSlotId: (id) => set({ pendingSlotId: id }),

  newGame: (opts, slotId) => {
    const s = createInitialState({ seed: String(Date.now()), ...opts });
    s.pace = useSettingsStore.getState().defaultPace;
    s.currentActivities = getEligibleActivities(s);
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    slots[slotId] = { id: slotId, name: (opts.companyName || 'Firma') + (opts.suffix ?? ''), createdAt: Date.now(), lastPlayed: Date.now(), state: s };
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ state: s, currentSlotId: slotId, screen: 'game', pendingSlotId: null, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },

  saveGame: () => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s || !get().currentSlotId) return;
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    const existing = slots[get().currentSlotId!];
    slots[get().currentSlotId!] = { id: get().currentSlotId!, name: existing?.name ?? (s.company.name + s.company.suffix), createdAt: existing?.createdAt ?? Date.now(), lastPlayed: Date.now(), state: s };
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },

  loadGame: (slotId) => {
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    const slot = slots[slotId];
    if (!slot) return false;
    slot.lastPlayed = Date.now();
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ state: slot.state, currentSlotId: slotId, screen: 'game', savesVersion: Date.now() });
    return true;
  },

  deleteSave: (slotId) => {
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    delete slots[slotId];
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },

  getSlots: () => loadSlotsFromStorage(),
  hasAnySave: () => Object.keys(loadSlotsFromStorage()).length > 0,
}));

import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { Pace } from '../engine/types';

const SETTINGS_KEY = 'pp-settings-v1';

export type ThemeId = 'default' | 'teletext' | 'newspaper' | 'gg' | 'brat';

export interface Settings {
  autosave: boolean;
  defaultPace: Pace;
  showHiddenStats: boolean;
  language: 'pl' | 'en';
  theme: ThemeId;
}

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: Settings = {
  autosave: true,
  defaultPace: 'quarter',
  showHiddenStats: false,
  language: 'pl',
  theme: 'default',
};

function loadSettings(): Settings {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
    return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
  }
}

function persist(s: Settings) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(s));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

interface SettingsStore extends Settings {
  set: <K extends keyof Settings>(key: K, value: Settings[K]) => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

export const useSettingsStore = create<SettingsStore>((set, get) => ({
  ...loadSettings(),
  set: (key, value) => {
    const next = { ...get(), [key]: value } as Settings;
    persist(next);
    set({ [key]: value } as any);
  },
  reset: () => {
    persist(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    set(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  },
}));

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import activitiesData from '../../data/activities.json';
import type { Activity, EventEffects } from '../../engine/types';
import { REPUTATION_LABELS } from '../ui-labels';

function describeEffects(e: EventEffects | undefined): string[] {
  if (!e) return [];
  const lines: string[] = [];
  if (e.stanKonta) {
    lines.push(`${e.stanKonta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${e.stanKonta.toLocaleString('pl-PL')} PLN`);
  }
  if (e.reputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.reputation)) {
      if (v) lines.push(`${REPUTATION_LABELS[k] ?? k}: ${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v}`);
    }
  }
  return lines;
}

export function ActivityScreen() {
  const { state, consumeActivity } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  const all = activitiesData as Activity[];
  const current = all.filter(a => state.currentActivities.includes(a.id));
  const alreadyChosen = !!state.chosenActivityThisTurn;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1">Aktywności</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
        {alreadyChosen
          ? `W tej turze wybrałeś już aktywność. Kliknij „Następna tura", aby przejść dalej.`
          : 'Wybierz jedną aktywność na tę turę. Wybór jest ostateczny.'}
      </p>
      <div className="grid gap-3">
        {current.map(a => {
          const chosen = state.chosenActivityThisTurn === a.id;
          const disabled = alreadyChosen;
          const effects = describeEffects(a.effects);
          return (
            <button
              key={a.id}
              onClick={() => !disabled && consumeActivity(a.id)}
              disabled={disabled}
              className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border-2 p-4 text-left transition ${
                chosen
                  ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-50'
                  : disabled
                  ? 'opacity-40 cursor-not-allowed border-transparent'
                  : 'border-transparent hover:border-stone-300'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="font-bold">
                {chosen ? '✓ ' : ''}
                {a.label}
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm text-stone-600 mt-1">{a.description}</div>
              {effects.length > 0 && (
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-2">
                  Skutki: {effects.join(' · ')}
                </div>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN, COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG, BRANCH_COST, COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';
import { makeRng } from '../../engine/rng';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';
import tradeData from '../../data/trade_data.json';
import type { ActivityCategory, CompanySize } from '../../engine/types';

export function ChangeActivityScreen() {
  const { 
    state, changeActivity, changeCompanySize, buildBranch, 
    buyGoods, sellGoods, resolveComplaint, buyAd, rentWarehouse
  } = useGameStore();
  
  if (!state) return null;

  const sizes: CompanySize[] = ['micro', 'small', 'medium', 'large', 'very_large', 'corporation'];
  const currentSizeIndex = sizes.indexOf(state.company.size);
  const nextSize = sizes[currentSizeIndex + 1];
  const nextConfig = nextSize ? COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG[nextSize] : null;

  const currentTrade = (tradeData as any).odziez;

  const { suppliers, buyers } = useMemo(() => {
    const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn + 77);
    const validSuppliers = currentTrade.suppliers.filter((s: any) => {
      if (s.req.minRepBranza && state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza < s.req.minRepBranza) return false;
      if (s.req.minRepMafia && state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < s.req.minRepMafia) return false;
      return true;
    });
    const validBuyers = currentTrade.buyers.filter((b: any) => {
      if (b.req.minRepPubliczna && state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna < b.req.minRepPubliczna) return false;
      return true;
    });
    return {
      suppliers: [...validSuppliers].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, 3),
      buyers: [...validBuyers].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, 3)
    };
  }, [state.seed, state.turn, state.company.reputation]);

  const canChange = state.turn - state.company.lastActivityChangeTurn >= ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN;

  const visibleActivities = useMemo(() => {
    const categories = branchesData as ActivityCategory[];
    const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn + 50);
    const mainCat = rng.pick(categories);
    const mainChoices = [...mainCat.activities].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, 3);
    const otherActivities = categories.filter(c => c.id !== mainCat.id).flatMap(c => c.activities);
    const extraCount = state.company.inventory.includes('komputer') ? 2 : 1;
    const extraChoices = [...otherActivities].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, extraCount);
    return [...mainChoices, ...extraChoices].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);
  }, [state.seed, state.turn, state.company.inventory]);

  // Obliczanie pojemności magazynu
  const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources || {}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const capacity = state.company.warehouseCapacity || 50;
  const isFull = currentStock >= capacity;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Zarządzanie Działalnością</h1>

      {/* REKLAMACJE */}
      {state.pendingComplaints?.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-red-800 mb-2">⚠ Reklamacje klientów</h2>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {state.pendingComplaints.map((comp: any) => (
              <div key={comp.id} className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center bg-white p-3 rounded shadow-sm gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="font-semibold text-sm">{comp.buyerName} zwraca towar</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">Zbyt niska jakość. Żądają zwrotu pieniędzy.</div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => resolveComplaint(comp.id, true)} className="bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold">Uznaj (-5k PLN)</button>
                  <button onClick={() => resolveComplaint(comp.id, false)} className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold">Odrzuć (-{comp.penalty} rep.)</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ROZMIAR FIRMY I FILIE */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 mb-6 border-l-4 border-stone-900">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Rozmiar: {COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.label}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">Filie: {state.company.branches}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {currentSizeIndex > 0 && (
              <button onClick={() => changeCompanySize('down')} className="px-4 py-2 border border-red-200 text-red-700 bg-red-50 rounded hover:bg-red-100 transition text-sm font-semibold">Zmniejsz</button>
            )}
            {nextConfig && (
              <button onClick={() => changeCompanySize('up')} disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < nextConfig.cost} className="px-4 py-2 bg-stone-900 text-white rounded hover:bg-stone-800 transition text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">
                Zwiększ na {COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[nextSize]?.label} ({nextConfig.cost.toLocaleString()} PLN)
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        {['large', 'very_large', 'corporation'].includes(state.company.size) && (
          <div className="border-t border-stone-200 pt-4 mt-2 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div><h3 className="font-bold text-sm">Ekspansja (Filie)</h3></div>
            <button onClick={buildBranch} disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < BRANCH_COST} className="px-4 py-2 bg-green-700 text-white rounded hover:bg-green-800 text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-50">Zbuduj filię ({BRANCH_COST.toLocaleString()} PLN)</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MAGAZYN */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 mb-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Magazyn: {currentStock} / {capacity} szt.</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">Pojemność magazynu pozwala na składowanie towarów.</p>
        </div>
        <button 
          onClick={rentWarehouse} 
          disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < 10000}
          className="px-4 py-2 border border-stone-300 text-stone-700 bg-stone-100 rounded hover:bg-stone-200 transition text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          Wynajmij +50 miejsca (10 000 PLN)
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* HANDEL */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-6">
        {/* ZAKUP */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Sprowadź towar</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {suppliers.map((sup: any) => (
              <div key={sup.id} className="border rounded p-3 hover:bg-stone-50 transition">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm">{sup.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">{sup.desc}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700">Jakość: {sup.quality}/10</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  <button onClick={() => buyGoods(sup, 1)} disabled={isFull || state.company.finances.stanKonta < sup.price} className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-50">1 szt.</button>
                  <button onClick={() => buyGoods(sup, 10)} disabled={currentStock + 10 > capacity || state.company.finances.stanKonta < sup.price * 9} className="flex-1 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-50">10 szt. (-10%)</button>
                  <button onClick={() => buyGoods(sup, 100)} disabled={currentStock + 100 > capacity || state.company.finances.stanKonta < sup.price * 75} className="flex-1 bg-blue-800 hover:bg-blue-900 text-white py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-50">100 szt. (-25%)</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SPRZEDAŻ */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Sprzedaj towar</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {buyers.map((buy: any) => (
              <div key={buy.id} className="border rounded p-3">
                <div className="font-bold text-sm">{buy.name}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 mb-2">{buy.desc} (x{buy.multiplier})</div>
                
                <div className="max-h-40 overflow-y-auto space-y-1 pr-1">
                  {Object.entries(state.company.resources || {}).map(([resId, amount]) => {
                    const amt = amount as number;
                    if (amt <= 0) return null;
                    const quality = parseInt(resId.split('_')[1]) || 1;
                    const baseVal = 100;
                    
                    return (
                      <div key={resId} className="flex gap-1 mb-1">
                        <div className="flex-1 text-xs bg-stone-100 p-1.5 rounded flex items-center">Jakość {quality} (Masz: {amt})</div>
                        <button onClick={() => sellGoods(buy, resId, 1, baseVal)} disabled={amt < 1} className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs disabled:opacity-50">1x</button>
                        <button onClick={() => sellGoods(buy, resId, 10, baseVal)} disabled={amt < 10} className="bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs disabled:opacity-50">10x</button>
                        <button onClick={() => sellGoods(buy, resId, 100, baseVal)} disabled={amt < 100} className="bg-green-800 hover:bg-green-900 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs disabled:opacity-50">100x</button>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                  {currentStock === 0 && <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 italic">Brak towaru</div>}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TRANSAKCJE W TOKU */}
      {state.pendingSales?.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-amber-800 mb-2">🚚 Transakcje w toku (rozliczenie w następnej turze)</h2>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            {state.pendingSales.map((sale: any) => (
              <div key={sale.id} className="text-sm bg-white p-2 rounded shadow-sm flex justify-between">
                <span>Sprzedaż {sale.amount} szt. (Jakość {sale.quality}) do: {sale.buyerName}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-amber-700">~{sale.estimatedRevenue.toLocaleString()} PLN</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* REKLAMY */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Kampanie Reklamowe</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => buyAd('radio')} disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < 5000} className="bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-left flex flex-col disabled:opacity-50"><span className="text-lg">📻 Radio</span><span className="text-xs text-stone-500">5 000 PLN</span></button>
          <button onClick={() => buyAd('internet')} disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < 15000} className="bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-left flex flex-col disabled:opacity-50"><span className="text-lg">🌐 Internet</span><span className="text-xs text-stone-500">15 000 PLN</span></button>
          <button onClick={() => buyAd('tv')} disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < 50000} className="bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-left flex flex-col disabled:opacity-50"><span className="text-lg">📺 Telewizja</span><span className="text-xs text-stone-500">50 000 PLN</span></button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ZMIANA BRANŻY */}
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-2">Zmiana profilu działalności</h2>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {visibleActivities.map(a => {
          const isCurrent = state.company.activity === a.id;
          return (
            <div key={a.id} className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 border-2 ${isCurrent ? 'border-stone-900 bg-stone-50' : 'border-transparent'}`}>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-3">
                <div><div className="font-bold text-stone-900">{a.name} {isCurrent && <span className="text-xs bg-stone-900 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ml-1">obecna</span>}</div></div>
                <button onClick={() => changeActivity(a.id)} disabled={isCurrent || !canChange} className="bg-stone-900 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm disabled:opacity-30">{isCurrent ? 'Wybrana' : 'Wybierz'}</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';

export function ChaseScreen() {
  const { state, chaseMove, dismissChase } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.activeChase) return null;
  const c = state.activeChase;

  const renderCell = (x: number, y: number) => {
    const isPlayer = c.player.x === x && c.player.y === y;
    const isEnemy = c.enemies.some(e => e.x === x && e.y === y);
    const isPainted = c.grid[y][x] === 1;

    let content = '';
    let bg = isPainted ? 'bg-green-800' : 'bg-stone-800'; // Zielone dla zamalowanych
    
    if (isPlayer) { content = '🏃'; bg = 'bg-blue-600'; }
    else if (isEnemy) { content = '🥷'; bg = 'bg-red-700'; }

    return (
      <div key={`${x}-${y}`} className={`aspect-square flex items-center justify-center text-lg md:text-2xl border border-stone-900 ${bg}`}>
        {content}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/90 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
      <div className="bg-stone-900 text-stone-100 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 border-4 border-red-900">
        
        <div className="text-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            Odstrasz mafię
          </h2>
          <p className="text-stone-400 text-sm">Tura {c.turn} / {c.maxTurns}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1">Zamaluj 40% planszy, zanim cię dopadną!</p>
        </div>

        {/* Plansza */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-8 gap-0.5 mb-6 bg-stone-950 p-1 rounded border border-stone-800">
          {Array.from({ length: c.gridSize }).map((_, y) => (
            Array.from({ length: c.gridSize }).map((_, x) => renderCell(x, y))
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Sterowanie */}
        {!c.finished ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => chaseMove(0, -1)} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl">↑</button>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => chaseMove(-1, 0)} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl">←</button>
              <button onClick={() => chaseMove(0, 1)} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl">↓</button>
              <button onClick={() => chaseMove(1, 0)} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl">→</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center">
            <div className={`text-xl font-bold mb-4 ${c.result === 'escaped' ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'}`}>
              {c.result === 'escaped' ? 'Zamalowałeś ich! Uciekli.' : 'Dopadli cię...'}
            </div>
            <button onClick={dismissChase} className="bg-stone-100 text-stone-900 w-full py-3 rounded font-bold hover:bg-white">
              Wróć do biura
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';

export function CombatScreen() {
  const { state, combatMove, dismissCombat } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.activeCombat) return null;
  const c = state.activeCombat;

  const canLawyer = state.company.finances.stanKonta >= 20000;
  const canInfluence = state.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy > 60;
  const canMafia = state.ceo.inMafia;  

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
      <div className="bg-stone-900 text-stone-100 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full p-6 border-4 border-stone-700">
        
        {/* Nagłówek */}
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            Wizyta Urzędnika
          </h2>
          <p className="text-stone-400 text-sm">Runda {c.round} / 10</p>
        </div>

        {/* Paski życia */}
        <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 mb-6">
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
              <span className="font-bold text-green-400">Twoja Siła Argumentów</span>
              <span>{c.playerHp} / {c.playerHpMax}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-4 bg-stone-800 rounded overflow-hidden">
              <div className="h-full bg-green-500 transition-all" style={{ width: `${(c.playerHp / c.playerHpMax) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
              <span className="font-bold text-red-400">Cierpliwość Urzędnika</span>
              <span>{c.enemyHp} / {c.enemyHpMax}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-4 bg-stone-800 rounded overflow-hidden">
              <div className="h-full bg-red-500 transition-all" style={{ width: `${(c.enemyHp / c.enemyHpMax) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Log walki */}
        <div className="bg-black/50 p-4 rounded h-48 overflow-y-auto mb-6 text-sm font-mono space-y-2">
          {c.log.map((entry, i) => (
            <div key={i} className={
              entry.type === 'player' ? 'text-green-300' :
              entry.type === 'enemy' ? 'text-red-300' : 'text-stone-400'
            }>
              {entry.type === 'player' ? '> ' : entry.type === 'enemy' ? '< ' : '* '}
              {entry.text}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Akcje / Koniec */}
        {!c.finished ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('wspolpraca')} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left">
              <div className="font-bold">Współpraca</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Zawsze działa. (-10 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('adwokat')} disabled={!canLawyer} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left disabled:opacity-50">
              <div className="font-bold">Wezwij adwokata</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Silny cios. (-20k PLN, -25 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('klamstwo')} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left">
              <div className="font-bold">Kłamstwo</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Bardzo silny cios. 30% szans na obrażenia własne. (-30 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('wplywy')} disabled={!canInfluence} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left disabled:opacity-50">
              <div className="font-bold">Wpływy</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Cios ostateczny. Wymaga opinii polityków &gt;60. (-40 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('mafia')} disabled={!canMafia} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left disabled:opacity-50">
              <div className="font-bold">Wpływy mafii</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Cios ostateczny. Wymaga bycia w mafii. (-50 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button onClick={dismissCombat} className="w-full bg-stone-100 text-stone-900 py-3 rounded font-bold text-lg hover:bg-white">
            Wyjdź z gabinetu
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\CompetitionScreen.tsx ===
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN } from '../../engine/competition';

export function CompetitionScreen() {
  const { state, interactCompetitor } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Konkurencja</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Wpływaj na rynek i dbaj o swoją pozycję.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm font-bold">
          Pozostałe interakcje: {state.interactionPoints} / {INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {(state.competitors ?? []).map((comp) => {
          const rep = Math.round(comp.reputation);
          return (
            <div key={comp.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{comp.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mb-3">{comp.description}</p>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className="text-stone-600">Ich reputacja:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold">{rep}%</span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded mb-4">
                  <div className="h-full bg-stone-400 rounded" style={{ width: `${rep}%` }} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => interactCompetitor(comp.id, 'positive')}
                  disabled={state.interactionPoints <= 0}
                  className="flex-1 bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-xs py-2 rounded font-semibold hover:bg-green-200 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Współpraca (+Ich rep, +Twoja)
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => interactCompetitor(comp.id, 'negative')}
                  disabled={state.interactionPoints <= 0}
                  className="flex-1 bg-red-100 text-red-800 text-xs py-2 rounded font-semibold hover:bg-red-200 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Sabotaż (Ryzyko)
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import type { Party } from '../../engine/types';
import { partyLabel } from '../ui-labels';

export function ElectionScreen({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.lastElectionResult) return null;

  const result = state.lastElectionResult;
  const allParties = partiesData as Party[];

  const sorted = Object.entries(result.seats)
    .sort(([, a], [, b]) => b - a)
    .map(([id, seats]) => ({
      id,
      seats,
      party: allParties.find(p => p.id === id),
    }));

  const totalSeats = Object.values(result.seats).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full p-8 my-8">
          <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-1">
          Wyniki wyborów
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-1">
          Wybory parlamentarne {result.year}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
          Nowy Sejm wybrany
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
          {sorted.map(({ id, seats, party }) => {
            const percent = (seats / totalSeats) * 100;
            const isMine = state.ceo.currentPartyId === id;
            const inCoalition = result.coalition.includes(id);
            return (
              <div key={id}>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className="font-semibold">
                    {party?.name ?? id}
                    {isMine && <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-stone-500">(twoja partia)</span>}
                    {inCoalition && <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-amber-600">· w koalicji</span>}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono">
                    {seats} mandatów · {percent.toFixed(1)}%
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-3 bg-stone-100 rounded overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full ${isMine ? 'bg-stone-900' : inCoalition ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-stone-400'}`}
                    style={{ width: `${percent}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="bg-stone-100 rounded p-4 mb-6 text-sm">
          <p className="mb-1">
            <b>Koalicja rządząca:</b> {result.coalition.map(partyLabel).join(', ') || 'brak'}
          </p>
          <p>
            <b>Nowy premier:</b> {partyLabel(result.premierId)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full bg-stone-900 text-white py-3 rounded font-bold hover:bg-stone-800"
        >
          OK
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { ContractType } from '../../engine/types';
import { WORKER_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';

export function EmployeesScreen() {
  const { state, hireWorker } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;
  const workers = state.company.employees;

  const getEfficiency = (count: number) => Math.max(20, Math.round((1 - (count * 0.05)) * 100));

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2">Pracownicy</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
        Każdy kolejny pracownik w danej grupie pracuje z mniejszą wydajnością (prawo malejących przychodów).
      </p>
      
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 mb-6">
        <h2 className="font-bold mb-3">Zatrudnienie</h2>
        {(['bezUmowy', 'zlecenie', 'uop'] as ContractType[]).map(c => {
          const count = workers[c];
          const nextWorkerEff = getEfficiency(count);
          
          return (
            <div key={c} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0">
              <div>
                <div className="font-semibold">
                  {c === 'bezUmowy' ? 'Bez umowy' : c === 'zlecenie' ? 'Umowa zlecenie' : 'Umowa o pracę'}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-600">
                  Koszt: {WORKER_CONFIG[c].wage} PLN | Baza: {WORKER_CONFIG[c].revenue} PLN
                </div>
                <div className={`text-xs font-semibold mt-1 ${nextWorkerEff < 50 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-green-700'}`}>
                  Wydajność kolejnego: {nextWorkerEff}%
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => hireWorker(c, -1)} disabled={count === 0} className="w-8 h-8 rounded border bg-white hover:bg-stone-50 disabled:opacity-30">−</button>
                <span className="w-10 text-center font-mono">{count}</span>
                <button onClick={() => hireWorker(c, 1)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded border bg-white hover:bg-stone-50">+</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
