=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\NewsScreen.tsx ===
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { NewsCategory, NewsItem } from '../../engine/types';

type Tab = 'news' | 'inbox';

const CATEGORY_COLORS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800',
  poll: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800',
  party: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
  economy: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800',
  custom: 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-700',
};

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'Plotka',
  poll: 'Sondaż',
  party: 'Partia',
  economy: 'Gospodarka',
  custom: 'Ciekawostka',
};

const NEWS_CATEGORIES: NewsCategory[] = ['poll', 'party', 'economy', 'rumor'];
const INBOX_CATEGORIES: NewsCategory[] = ['custom'];

export function NewsScreen() {
    const { state, markNewsRead, resolveNewsChoice, devAddTestNews } = useGameStore();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('inbox');
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!state) return null;

  const allItems = state.news;
  const filtered = allItems.filter(n =>
    tab === 'news' ? NEWS_CATEGORIES.includes(n.category) : INBOX_CATEGORIES.includes(n.category)
  );

  const selected: NewsItem | null =
    filtered.find(n => n.id === selectedId) ?? filtered[0] ?? null;

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedId(null);
  }, [tab]);

  // Auto-mark as read when opened, ale tylko jeśli nie ma pending action
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selected && !selected.read && !(selected.choices && !selected.resolved)) {
      markNewsRead(selected.id);
    }
  }, [selected?.id]);

  const handleSelect = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedId(id);
  };

  const newsCount = allItems.filter(n => NEWS_CATEGORIES.includes(n.category)).length;
  const inboxCount = allItems.filter(n => INBOX_CATEGORIES.includes(n.category)).length;

  const unreadNews = allItems.filter(n =>
    NEWS_CATEGORIES.includes(n.category) &&
    (!n.read || (n.choices && !n.resolved))
  ).length;
  const unreadInbox = allItems.filter(n =>
    INBOX_CATEGORIES.includes(n.category) &&
    (!n.read || (n.choices && !n.resolved))
  ).length;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1">Korespondencja</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-4">
        Doniesienia z ostatnich kwartałów. Wiadomości z akcją wymagają decyzji.
      </p>

     <button
      onClick={devAddTestNews}
      className="mb-4 bg-red-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-xs"
     >
        🧪 DEV: Dodaj testową wiadomość
     </button>

      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
        <TabButton active={tab === 'inbox'} onClick={() => setTab('inbox')} unread={unreadInbox}>
          ✉ Skrzynka odbiorcza {inboxCount > 0 && <span className="opacity-60 ml-1">({inboxCount})</span>}
        </TabButton>
        <TabButton active={tab === 'news'} onClick={() => setTab('news')} unread={unreadNews}>
          📰 Newsy {newsCount > 0 && <span className="opacity-60 ml-1">({newsCount})</span>}
        </TabButton>
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-8 text-center text-stone-500">
          {tab === 'news'
            ? 'Brak doniesień. Świat jeszcze nie zauważył twojej firmy.'
            : 'Skrzynka odbiorcza jest pusta.'}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {/* Lista po lewej */}
          <div className="md:col-span-1 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
            <div className="max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto">
              {filtered.map(item => {
                const isSelected = selected?.id === item.id;
                const hasPendingAction = !!(item.choices && !item.resolved);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.id}
                    onClick={() => handleSelect(item.id)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-3 border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 transition ${
                      isSelected ? 'bg-stone-100 border-l-4 border-l-stone-900' : 'hover:bg-stone-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold ${CATEGORY_COLORS[item.category]}`}>
                        {CATEGORY_LABELS[item.category]}
                      </span>
                      {hasPendingAction && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold bg-red-500 text-white">
                          AKCJA
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-mono">
                        {item.year} Q{item.quarter}
                      </span>
                      {(!item.read || hasPendingAction) && (
                        <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ml-auto ${hasPendingAction ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-blue-500'}`} />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className={`text-sm ${isSelected ? 'font-bold' : item.read && !hasPendingAction ? 'font-medium' : 'font-bold'} text-stone-900`}>
                      {item.headline}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Treść po prawej */}
          <div className="md:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 md:p-6">
            {selected ? (
              <article>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 flex-wrap">
                  <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold ${CATEGORY_COLORS[selected.category]}`}>
                    {CATEGORY_LABELS[selected.category]}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-mono">
                    {selected.year} Q{selected.quarter}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-3">{selected.headline}</h2>
                <p className="text-stone-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                  {selected.body ?? 'Brak dodatkowych szczegółów.'}
                </p>

                {/* Decyzje — jeśli są i nie rozwiązane */}
                {selected.choices && !selected.resolved && (
                  <div className="mt-6 border-t border-stone-200 pt-4">
                    <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-3">
                      Wymaga decyzji
                      {selected.expiresAtTurn !== undefined && (
                        <span className="ml-2 text-red-600">
                          (wygasa za {Math.max(0, selected.expiresAtTurn - state.turn)} tur)
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                      {selected.choices.map(c => (
                        <button
                          key={c.id}
                          onClick={() => resolveNewsChoice(selected.id, c.id)}
                          className="text-left border-2 border-stone-200 rounded-lg p-3 hover:border-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-50 transition"
                        >
                          <div className="font-semibold">{c.label}</div>
                          {c.description && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-stone-600 mt-1">{c.description}</div>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Po decyzji — pokaż co wybrałeś */}
                {selected.resolved && selected.resolvedChoiceId && (
                  <div className="mt-6 border-t border-stone-200 pt-4">
                    {selected.resolvedChoiceId === '__expired__' ? (
                      <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 italic">
                        Zignorowano. Sprawa się przedawniła.
                      </p>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 italic">
                        Decyzja: {selected.choices?.find(c => c.id === selected.resolvedChoiceId)?.label ?? selected.resolvedChoiceId}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </article>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-stone-500 text-sm">Wybierz wiadomość z listy po lewej.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

  );

  label,

}: {

function Toggle({ value, onChange }: { value: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => onChange(!value)}
      className={`w-12 h-6 rounded-full transition relative ${
        value ? 'bg-stone-900' : 'bg-stone-300'
      }`}
    >
      <span
        className={`absolute top-0.5 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white transition ${
          value ? 'left-6' : 'left-0.5'
        }`}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

  active,

}: {

  onClick: () => void;

        active ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900' : 'bg-white hover:bg-stone-50'
      }`}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { getPartySize } from '../../engine/politics';
import { makeRng } from '../../engine/rng';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import type { Party } from '../../engine/types';

const IDEOLOGY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  radical_right: 'Skrajna prawica',
  moderate_right: 'Umiarkowana prawica',
  center: 'Centrum',
  moderate_left: 'Umiarkowana lewica',
  radical_left: 'Skrajna lewica',
};

const SIZE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  very_small: 'Bardzo mała',
  small: 'Mała',
  medium: 'Średnia',
  large: 'Duża',
  very_large: 'Bardzo duża',
};

export function PartyScreen() {
  const { state, joinParty } = useGameStore();

  if (!state) return null;

  const is1989 = state.year <= 1989;
  const switchesLeft = 3 - state.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[state.phase];
  const myParty = (partiesData as Party[]).find(p => p.id === state.ceo.currentPartyId) ?? null;

  // Ustalamy limit partii (bazowo 4, z komputerem 6)
  const maxChoices = state.company.inventory.includes('komputer') ? 6 : 4;

  // Logika losowania partii (zapewniająca różnorodność)
  const visibleParties = useMemo(() => {
    if (is1989) return []; // W 1989 nie ma wyboru

    // 1. Pobieramy wszystkie dostępne partie
    const active = (partiesData as Party[]).filter(p => {
      if (p.isPSL && !state.politics.pslAvailable) return false;
      const sched = state.politics.partySchedule[p.id];
      const isYearValid = sched ? (state.year >= sched.from && state.year <= sched.to) : (state.year >= p.activeFrom && state.year <= p.activeTo);
      if (!isYearValid) return false;

      if (p.pzprAvailable !== 'both') {
        const playerIsPzpr = state.ceo.party === 'PZPR';
        if (playerIsPzpr && p.pzprAvailable === 'no') return false;
        if (!playerIsPzpr && p.pzprAvailable === 'yes') return false;
      }
      return true;
    });

    // 2. Dzielimy na pule ideologiczne
    const left = active.filter(p => ['radical_left', 'moderate_left'].includes(p.ideology));
    const center = active.filter(p => p.ideology === 'center');
    const right = active.filter(p => ['radical_right', 'moderate_right'].includes(p.ideology));

    // 3. Używamy seedowanego RNG, żeby lista nie zmieniała się przy każdym kliknięciu (tylko co turę)
    const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 13 + 7);
    const selected: Party[] = [];

    const addFromPool = (pool: Party[]) => {
      if (selected.length >= maxChoices || pool.length === 0) return;
      const shuffled = [...pool].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);
      const pick = shuffled.find(p => !selected.some(s => s.id === p.id));
      if (pick) selected.push(pick);
    };

    // Gwarantujemy po jednej z każdej opcji (jeśli istnieją)
    addFromPool(left);
    addFromPool(center);
    addFromPool(right);

    // Dobijamy resztę wolnymi miejscami (z puli pozostałych partii)
    const remaining = active.filter(p => !selected.some(s => s.id === p.id));
    const shuffledRemaining = [...remaining].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);
    
    while (selected.length < maxChoices && shuffledRemaining.length > 0) {
      selected.push(shuffledRemaining.pop()!);
    }

    // Mieszamy ostateczną listę, żeby kolejność na ekranie była losowa
    return selected.sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);
  }, [state.year, state.turn, state.ceo.party, state.politics.pslAvailable, maxChoices, is1989]);

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Partie polityczne</h1>

      {is1989 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 text-amber-900 p-4 rounded-lg mb-6">
          <p className="font-bold">Okres przejściowy</p>
          <p className="text-sm">W 1989 roku trwa Sejm kontraktowy. Dołączanie do partii politycznych będzie możliwe po pierwszych wolnych wyborach w 1990 roku.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {myParty && (
        <section className="mb-8">
          <h2 className="text-sm uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-2">Twoja partia</h2>
          <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white rounded-lg p-5">
            <div className="font-bold text-lg">{myParty.name}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-stone-300 mb-3">
              {IDEOLOGY_LABELS[myParty.ideology]} · {SIZE_LABELS[getPartySize(myParty, state.year)]}
              {state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(myParty.id) && ' · w koalicji rządzącej'}
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 italic mb-4">"{myParty.descriptionAfterJoin}"</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 border-t border-stone-700 pt-2">
              W tej fazie zmieniłeś partię {state.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[state.phase]} / 3 razy.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {!myParty && !is1989 && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 mb-8 text-stone-600">
          <p>Nie należysz do żadnej partii.</p>
          <p className="text-xs mt-1">Możesz zmienić partię jeszcze {switchesLeft} razy w tej fazie.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {!is1989 && (
        <section>
          <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500">
              Dostępne opcje (rok {state.year})
            </h2>
            {maxChoices > 4 && (
              <span className="text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold bg-green-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                Dzięki komputerowi widzisz więcej partii
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {visibleParties.map(p => {
              const isMine = p.id === state.ceo.currentPartyId;
              const isRuling = state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(p.id);
              const currentSize = getPartySize(p, state.year);
              const randomDesc = p.descriptions[Math.floor(Math.random() * p.descriptions.length)];

              return (
                <div
                  key={p.id}
                  className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 border-2 flex flex-col justify-between transition ${
                    isMine ? 'border-stone-900 ring-2 ring-stone-900/20' : isRuling ? 'border-amber-500' : 'border-transparent hover:border-stone-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                      <h3 className="font-bold leading-tight">{p.name}</h3>
                      <div className="flex gap-1 shrink-0">
                        {isMine && <span className="text-[10px] bg-stone-900 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">twoja</span>}
                        {isRuling && !isMine && <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-500 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">rządzi</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-semibold mb-2">
                      {IDEOLOGY_LABELS[p.ideology]} · {SIZE_LABELS[currentSize]}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 italic mb-4">
                      "{randomDesc}"
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => joinParty(p.id)}
                    disabled={switchesLeft <= 0 || isMine}
                    className="text-sm bg-stone-900 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded disabled:opacity-30 w-full mt-auto"
                  >
                    {isMine ? 'Należysz' : 'Dołącz'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\PresidentialElectionScreen.tsx ===
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import type { Party } from '../../engine/types';

function displayPartyLabel(partyId: string | null, partyLabel: string | undefined): string {
  if (partyLabel) return partyLabel;
  if (!partyId) return 'niezależny';
  return partyId.replace(/_/g, ' ');
}

export function PresidentialElectionScreen({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.lastPresidentialResult) return null;

  const r = state.lastPresidentialResult;
  const allParties = partiesData as Party[];

  const firstRoundSorted = [...r.candidates].sort((a, b) => b.votesPercent - a.votesPercent);

  const partyNameFor = (partyId: string | null) => {
  if (!partyId) return 'niezależny';
  const p = allParties.find(x => x.id === partyId);
  if (p) return p.name;
  return partyId.replace(/_/g, ' ');
};

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full p-8 my-8">
        <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-1">
          Wyniki wyborów
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-1">
          Wybory prezydenckie {r.year}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
          {r.secondRound ? 'Rozstrzygnięcie w II turze' : 'Rozstrzygnięcie w I turze'}
        </p>

        <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white rounded-lg p-5 mb-6">
          <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-400 mb-1">
            {r.secondRound ? 'Zwycięzca II tury' : 'Zwycięzca'}
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{r.winner.name}</div>
          <div className="text-sm text-stone-300">
            {partyNameFor(r.winner.partyId)}
          </div>
        </div>

        <h2 className="font-bold mb-3 text-stone-800">I tura</h2>
        <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
          {firstRoundSorted.map(c => {
            const isWinner = c.id === r.winner.id;
            const inSecondRound = r.secondRoundCandidates?.some(x => x.id === c.id);
            return (
              <div key={c.id}>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className={isWinner ? 'font-bold' : ''}>
                    {c.name}
                    <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-stone-500">
                      {partyNameFor(c.partyId)}
                    </span>
                    {inSecondRound && (
                      <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-amber-600">· II tura</span>
                    )}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono">{c.votesPercent.toFixed(1)}%</span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-2 bg-stone-100 rounded overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full ${isWinner ? 'bg-stone-900' : inSecondRound ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-stone-400'}`}
                    style={{ width: `${c.votesPercent}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {r.secondRound && r.secondRoundCandidates && (
          <>
            <h2 className="font-bold mb-3 text-stone-800">II tura</h2>
            <div className="bg-stone-50 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6 text-sm">
              <p className="mb-2 text-stone-600">
                Żaden kandydat nie zdobył ponad 50% w I turze. Do II tury weszli:
              </p>
              {r.secondRoundCandidates.map(c => (
                <div key={c.id} className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-stone-200 last:border-b-0">
                  <span className={c.id === r.winner.id ? 'font-bold' : ''}>
                    {c.name} {c.id === r.winner.id && '(zwycięzca)'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono">{partyNameFor(c.partyId)}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full bg-stone-900 text-white py-3 rounded font-bold hover:bg-stone-800"
        >
          OK
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\RankingsScreen.tsx ===
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { computeRanking, SIZE_ORDER } from '../../engine/competition';
import type { RankingRow } from '../../engine/competition';
import { COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';
import type { Trend } from '../../engine/types';

type SortKey = 'rank' | 'name' | 'size' | 'reputation' | 'value' | 'condition' | 'opinion';
type SortDir = 'asc' | 'desc';

const COLUMNS: { key: SortKey; label: string; align: 'left' | 'right' }[] = [
  { key: 'rank', label: '#', align: 'left' },
  { key: 'name', label: 'Firma', align: 'left' },
  { key: 'size', label: 'Rozmiar', align: 'left' },
  { key: 'reputation', label: 'Reputacja', align: 'right' },
  { key: 'value', label: 'Wartość', align: 'right' },
  { key: 'condition', label: 'Kondycja', align: 'right' },
  { key: 'opinion', label: 'Opinia o Tobie', align: 'right' },
];

function sortValue(row: RankingRow, key: SortKey): number | string {
  switch (key) {
    case 'rank': return row.rank;
    case 'name': return row.name.toLocaleLowerCase('pl');
    case 'size': return SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(row.size);
    case 'reputation': return row.reputation;
    case 'value': return row.value;
    case 'condition': return row.condition;
    case 'opinion': return row.opinionOfPlayer ?? -1;
  }
}

function formatValue(v: number): string {
  if (v >= 1e9) return `${(v / 1e9).toFixed(1)} mld`;
  if (v >= 1e6) return `${(v / 1e6).toFixed(1)} mln`;
  if (v >= 1e3) return `${Math.round(v / 1e3).toLocaleString('pl-PL')} tys.`;
  return v.toLocaleString('pl-PL');
}

function TrendMark({ t }: { t: Trend }) {
  if (t === 1) return <span aria-label="Wzrost">⬆️</span>;
  if (t === -1) return <span aria-label="Spadek">⬇️</span>;
  return <span aria-label="Bez zmian" className="opacity-40">➡️</span>;
}

export function RankingsScreen() {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<{ key: SortKey; dir: SortDir }>({ key: 'rank', dir: 'asc' });

  const rows = useMemo(() => (state ? computeRanking(state) : []), [state]);

  const sortedRows = useMemo(() => {
    const factor = sort.dir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
    return [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
      const va = sortValue(a, sort.key);
      const vb = sortValue(b, sort.key);
      const cmp = typeof va === 'string' && typeof vb === 'string' ? va.localeCompare(vb, 'pl') : (va as number) - (vb as number);
      return cmp * factor || a.rank - b.rank;
    });
  }, [rows, sort]);

  if (!state) return null;

  const me = rows.find((r) => r.isPlayer);
  const prevRank = state.playerPrev?.rank ?? null;
  const rankDelta = me && prevRank !== null ? prevRank - me.rank : 0;

  const toggleSort = (key: SortKey) => {
    setSort((cur) =>
      cur.key === key
        ? { key, dir: cur.dir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' }
        : { key, dir: key === 'rank' || key === 'name' ? 'asc' : 'desc' },
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between sm:items-end gap-2 mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Rankingi</h1>
        {me && (
          <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm font-bold self-start sm:self-auto">
            Twoje miejsce: {me.rank} / {rows.length}
            {rankDelta !== 0 && (
              <span className="ml-2">{rankDelta > 0 ? `⬆️ +${rankDelta}` : `⬇️ ${rankDelta}`}</span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[640px]">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-stone-200 text-stone-600">
              {COLUMNS.map((col) => {
                const active = sort.key === col.key;
                return (
                  <th
                    key={col.key}
                    scope="col"
                    aria-sort={active ? (sort.dir === 'asc' ? 'ascending' : 'descending') : 'none'}
                    className={`p-0 font-semibold ${col.align === 'right' ? 'text-right' : 'text-left'}`}
                  >
                    <button
                      onClick={() => toggleSort(col.key)}
                      className={`w-full px-3 py-2 hover:bg-stone-50 ${col.align === 'right' ? 'text-right' : 'text-left'} ${active ? 'text-stone-900' : ''}`}
                    >
                      {col.label}
                      {active && <span className="ml-1">{sort.dir === 'asc' ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>}
                    </button>
                  </th>
                );
              })}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {sortedRows.map((row) => (
              <tr
                key={row.id}
                className={`border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 ${row.isPlayer ? 'bg-amber-50 font-semibold' : ''}`}
              >
                <td className="px-3 py-2 font-mono">{row.rank}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2">{row.name}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2">{COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[row.size]?.label ?? row.size}</td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                  {Math.round(row.reputation)}% <TrendMark t={row.trend.reputation} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right whitespace-nowrap font-mono">
                  {formatValue(row.value)} <TrendMark t={row.trend.value} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                  {Math.round(row.condition)}% <TrendMark t={row.trend.condition} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                  {row.opinionOfPlayer === null ? (
                    <span className="text-stone-400">—</span>
                  ) : (
                    `${Math.round(row.opinionOfPlayer)}%`
                  )}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\SavesScreen.tsx ===
import { useGameStore, SLOT_IDS, MAX_SLOTS } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { SaveSlot } from '../../store/autosave';

export function SavesScreen() {
  const {
    savesMode, pendingSlotId, savesVersion,
    setScreen, setPendingSlotId,
    loadGame, deleteSave, getSlots,
  } = useGameStore();

  // savesVersion wymusza rerender po delete/save
  void savesVersion;

  const slots = getSlots();
  const title = savesMode === 'new' ? 'Wybierz slot na nową grę' : 'Wczytaj zapis';

  const formatDate = (ts: number) => {
    const d = new Date(ts);
    const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${pad(d.getDate())}.${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}.${d.getFullYear()} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
  };

  const handleClick = (slotId: string) => {
    const slot = slots[slotId];
    if (savesMode === 'new') {
      if (slot) {
        const confirmed = confirm(`Slot "${slot.name}" jest zajęty (${slot.state.year} Q${slot.state.quarter}). Nadpisać?`);
        if (!confirmed) return;
      }
      setPendingSlotId(slotId);
      setScreen('start');
    } else {
      if (!slot) return;
      loadGame(slotId);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = (e: React.MouseEvent, slotId: string) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const slot = slots[slotId];
    if (!slot) return;
    const confirmed = confirm(`Usunąć zapis "${slot.name}"? Nie da się cofnąć.`);
    if (confirmed) deleteSave(slotId);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 p-6 flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="max-w-3xl w-full bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg p-6 md:p-8">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{title}</h1>
          <button
            onClick={() => setScreen('menu')}
            className="text-sm text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-900"
          >
            ← Powrót
          </button>
        </div>

        {savesMode === 'new' && pendingSlotId && (
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mb-3">
            Wybrano slot — kontynuuj w ekranie startowym.
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {SLOT_IDS.map((slotId, idx) => {
            const slot: SaveSlot | null = slots[slotId] ?? null;
            return (
              <div
                key={slotId}
                className={`border-2 rounded-lg p-4 transition ${
                  slot
                    ? savesMode === 'load'
                      ? 'border-stone-300 hover:border-stone-900 cursor-pointer'
                      : 'border-stone-300 hover:border-stone-900'
                    : 'border-dashed border-stone-300'
                }`}
                onClick={() => handleClick(slotId)}
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 font-semibold">
                    Slot {idx + 1}
                  </div>
                  {slot && (
                    <button
                      onClick={(e) => handleDelete(e, slotId)}
                      className="text-xs text-red-600 hover:text-red-800"
                    >
                      Usuń
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {slot ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="font-bold text-stone-900">
                      {slot.state.company.name}{slot.state.company.suffix}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-1">
                      Rok {slot.state.year} Q{slot.state.quarter} · {slot.state.phase}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-mono">
                      Ostatnio: {formatDate(slot.lastPlayed)}
                    </div>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <div className="text-sm text-stone-400 italic">
                    Pusty slot
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 mt-4 text-center">
          Maksymalnie {MAX_SLOTS} zapisów.
          {savesMode === 'load' && !Object.keys(slots).length && ' Brak zapisów do wczytania.'}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

          ))}
        </div>

  );

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { makeRng } from '../../engine/rng';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import { partyLabel } from '../ui-labels';

const IDEOLOGY_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  radical_left: 'lewica', moderate_left: 'lewica', center: 'centrum',
  moderate_right: 'prawica', radical_right: 'prawica'
};

export function StatsScreen() {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  const hasTygodnik = state.company.inventory.includes('tygodnik_opinii');
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 13 + 7);
  const macro = state.macroStats || { unemployment: 10, development: 40 }; // Zabezpieczenie

  const presId = state.politics.president?.partyId;
  const premId = state.politics.premierId;
  let cohesion = 0;
  let cohesionText = 'Brak rządu';
  
  if (presId && premId) {
    if (presId === premId) { cohesion = 100; cohesionText = 'Pełna (ta sama partia)'; }
    else {
      const presParty = (partiesData as any[]).find(p => p.id === presId);
      const premParty = (partiesData as any[]).find(p => p.id === premId);
      if (presParty && premParty) {
        const presIdeology = IDEOLOGY_MAP[presParty.ideology];
        const premIdeology = IDEOLOGY_MAP[premParty.ideology];
        if (presIdeology === premIdeology) { cohesion = 60; cohesionText = 'Umiarkowana (ta sama strona)'; }
        else { cohesion = 20; cohesionText = 'Niska (konflikt)'; }
      }
    }
  }

  const rulingParty = (partiesData as any[]).find(p => p.id === premId);
  const econView = rulingParty?.economicView ?? 0;
  const programText = econView > 0 ? `Wolnorynkowy (+${econView})` : econView < 0 ? `Socjalny (${econView})` : 'Centrowy';

  const allCategories = branchesData as any[];
  const promCat = rng.pick(allCategories);
  const unpromCat = rng.pick(allCategories.filter(c => c.id !== promCat.id));

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Statystyki Globalne</h1>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-6">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Sytuacja Polityczna</h2>
          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-stone-500 mb-1">Prezydent: <b className="text-stone-900">{state.politics.president?.name || 'Brak'}</b></div>
            <div className="text-sm text-stone-500 mb-3">Premier: <b className="text-stone-900">{partyLabel(premId)}</b></div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
              <span>Zgodność rządu</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{cohesionText}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded">
              <div className={`h-full rounded ${cohesion > 80 ? 'bg-green-500' : cohesion > 40 ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-red-500'}`} style={{ width: `${cohesion}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="text-sm mb-1">Program rządu: <b className="text-stone-900">{programText}</b></div>
            <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded relative">
              <div className="absolute top-0 bottom-0 w-2 bg-stone-900 rounded-full" style={{ left: `${((econView + 2) / 4) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-stone-400 mt-1">
              <span>Socjalizm</span>
              <span>Wolny Rynek</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Gospodarka</h2>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Rozwój kraju</span>
                <span>{macro.development}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded"><div className="h-full bg-blue-500 rounded" style={{ width: `${macro.development}%` }} /></div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Bezrobocie</span>
                <span>{macro.unemployment}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded"><div className="h-full bg-red-500 rounded" style={{ width: `${macro.unemployment}%` }} /></div>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1">
                {macro.unemployment > 15 ? 'Wysokie bezrobocie: łatwiej o tanich pracowników.' : 'Niskie bezrobocie: pracownicy będą żądać lepszych umów.'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Prognozy rynkowe</h2>
      {!hasTygodnik ? (
        <div className="bg-stone-100 border border-stone-300 p-4 rounded-lg text-center text-stone-500 text-sm">
          Kup "Tygodnik Opinii" w zakładce Biuro, aby odblokować prognozy i dodatkowe statystyki.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 p-4 rounded-lg">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-green-800 mb-1">Perspektywiczna branża</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-green-700">W tym roku rynek łaskawie patrzy na: <b>{promCat.name}</b>. Inwestycja tutaj da dodatkowe premie i oferty.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 p-4 rounded-lg">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-red-800 mb-1">Nierokująca branża</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-red-700">Unikaj w tym roku: <b>{unpromCat.name}</b>. Panuje tam zastój i trudno o zysk.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../store/gameStore';
import { StatisticsScreen } from './screens/StatisticsScreen';
import { PartyScreen } from './screens/PartyScreen';
import { ActivityScreen } from './screens/ActivityScreen';
import { EventScreen } from './screens/EventScreen';
import { ConsequenceScreen } from './screens/ConsequenceScreen';
import { ElectionScreen } from './screens/ElectionScreen';
import { PresidentialElectionScreen } from './screens/PresidentialElectionScreen';
import { NewsScreen } from './screens/NewsScreen';
import { ChangeActivityScreen } from './screens/ChangeActivityScreen';
import { CombatScreen } from './screens/CombatScreen';
import { EmployeesScreen } from './screens/EmployeesScreen';
import { OfficeScreen } from './screens/OfficeScreen';
import { RankingsScreen } from './screens/RankingsScreen';
import { AchievementsScreen } from './screens/AchievementsScreen';
import { IntroScreen } from './screens/IntroScreen';
import { ChaseScreen } from './screens/ChaseScreen';
import { StatsScreen } from './screens/StatsScreen';
import { CompetitionScreen } from './screens/CompetitionScreen';
import { MafiaActivityScreen } from './screens/MafiaActivityScreen';
import { OptionsModal } from './components/OptionsModal';

import { COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from '../engine/constants';
import { getActivityFullInfo } from '../engine/activityHelper';

type MainTab = 'main' | 'news' | 'management' | 'global';
type ManagementTab = 'changeActivity' | 'party' | 'employees' | 'office' | 'activity' | 'statistics' | 'mafiaActivity' | 'competition';
type GlobalTab = 'rankings' | 'achievements' | 'stats';

export function Dashboard() {
  const {
    state, doTurn, takeEmergencyLoan, dismissElection, dismissPresidentialResult,
  } = useGameStore();
  
  const [mainTab, setMainTab] = useState<MainTab>('main');
  const [mgmtTab, setMgmtTab] = useState<ManagementTab>('changeActivity');
  const [globalTab, setGlobalTab] = useState<GlobalTab>('rankings');
  const [showOptions, setShowOptions] = useState(false);

  if (!state) return <div className="p-8">Ładowanie...</div>;

  if (state.showIntro) return <IntroScreen />;
  if (state.lastPresidentialResult) return <PresidentialElectionScreen onClose={dismissPresidentialResult} />;
  if (state.lastElectionResult) return <ElectionScreen onClose={dismissElection} />;
  if (state.lastChoiceResult) return <ConsequenceScreen />;
  if (state.pendingEvents.length > 0) return <EventScreen />;
  if (state.activeCombat) return <CombatScreen />;
  if (state.activeChase) return <ChaseScreen />;

  if (state.gameOver) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-stone-100">
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-w-md text-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2">Koniec gry</h1>
          <p className="mb-4">{state.gameOver.reason}</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">Przetrwałeś do {state.year} Q{state.quarter}.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const party = partyLabel(state.ceo.currentPartyId);
  const chosenActivityLabel = state.chosenActivityThisTurn ?? 'brak';
  const unreadCount = state.news.filter(n => !n.read).length;
  const activityInfo = getActivityFullInfo(state.company.activity);
  const sizeLabel = COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.label || state.company.size;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 p-4 md:p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between md:items-end gap-3 mb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold truncate max-w-[300px] sm:max-w-md md:max-w-xl" title={`${state.company.name}${state.company.suffix}`}>
            {state.company.name}{state.company.suffix}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">{state.year} Q{state.quarter} · {state.phase} {state.ceo.inMafia && ' · <mafia>'}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4">
          <div className="text-left md:text-right">
            <p className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-mono">{state.company.finances.stanKonta.toLocaleString('pl-PL')} PLN</p>
            <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-stone-600">Reputacja: {Math.round(state.company.reputation.ogolna)}%</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setShowOptions(true)} className="bg-white border px-3 py-2 rounded text-sm hover:bg-stone-50 shrink-0">⚙</button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {unreadCount > 0 && mainTab !== 'news' && (
        <button onClick={() => setMainTab('news')} className="w-full bg-blue-100 border border-blue-300 text-blue-900 rounded-lg p-3 mb-4 text-left hover:bg-blue-200 transition flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="text-2xl">✉</span>
          <div>
            <div className="font-bold">Masz {unreadCount} nieprzeczytanych wiadomości</div>
            <div className="text-xs">Kliknij, aby przejść do skrzynki →</div>
          </div>
        </button>
      )}

      <nav className="flex gap-2 mb-3 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        <NavButton active={mainTab === 'main'} onClick={() => setMainTab('main')}>Główny</NavButton>
        <NavButton active={mainTab === 'news'} onClick={() => setMainTab('news')}>Wiadomości</NavButton>
        <NavButton active={mainTab === 'management'} onClick={() => setMainTab('management')}>Zarządzanie</NavButton>
        <NavButton active={mainTab === 'global'} onClick={() => setMainTab('global')}>Globalne</NavButton>
      </nav>

      {/* ZARZĄDZANIE - tu jest Konkurencja i Polityka */}
      {mainTab === 'management' && (
        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 border-b border-stone-300 pb-2 overflow-x-auto">
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'changeActivity'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('changeActivity')}>Działalność</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'party'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('party')}>Polityka</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'competition'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('competition')}>Konkurencja</SubNavButton> {/* <-- TUTAJ */}
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'employees'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('employees')}>Pracownicy</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'office'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('office')}>Biuro</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'activity'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('activity')}>Aktywności</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'statistics'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('statistics')}>Statystyki</SubNavButton>
          
          {state.ceo.inMafia && (
            <SubNavButton active={mgmtTab === 'mafiaActivity'} onClick={() => setMgmtTab('mafiaActivity')}>Aktywność mafijna</SubNavButton>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* GLOBALNE - tu jest tylko Rankingi, Osiągnięcia i Makro */}
      {mainTab === 'global' && (
        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 border-b border-stone-300 pb-2 overflow-x-auto">
          <SubNavButton active={globalTab === 'rankings'} onClick={() => setGlobalTab('rankings')}>Rankingi</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={globalTab === 'achievements'} onClick={() => setGlobalTab('achievements')}>Osiągnięcia</SubNavButton>
          <SubNavButton active={globalTab === 'stats'} onClick={() => setGlobalTab('stats')}>Makro</SubNavButton>
        </div>
      )}

      {mainTab === 'main' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">
          <InfoCard label="Działalność" value={activityInfo.subcategory || state.company.activity} />
          <InfoCard label="Rozmiar" value={sizeLabel} />
          <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white rounded-lg shadow-md p-3 border-b-4 border-amber-500 flex flex-col justify-center">
            <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-400 mb-1">Kondycja</div>
            <div className="text-2xl leading-none flex items-center h-6">
              {state.profitabilityTrend === 'up2' ? '⬆️⬆️' :
               state.profitabilityTrend === 'up1' ? '⬆️' :
               state.profitabilityTrend === 'down2' ? '⬇️⬇️' :
               state.profitabilityTrend === 'down1' ? '⬇️' : '➡️'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {state.bankruptWarningTurns > 0 && state.company.finances.stanKonta < 0 && (
        <div className="bg-red-100 border border-red-500 p-3 rounded mb-4 mt-4">
          <p className="font-bold">⚠ Bankructwo za {2 - state.bankruptWarningTurns} tur!</p>
          <button onClick={takeEmergencyLoan} className="mt-2 bg-red-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded">Weź kredyt ratunkowy (50 000 PLN, 15%)</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-4">
        {mainTab === 'main' && (
          <div>
            <OfficeView companyName={`${state.company.name}${state.company.suffix}`} year={state.year} quarter={state.quarter} inMafia={state.ceo.inMafia} inventory={state.company.inventory} />
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch mt-2">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 flex-1">
                <InfoCard label="Aktywność w tej turze" value={chosenActivityLabel} muted={!state.chosenActivityThisTurn} />
                <InfoCard label="Partia" value={party} />
                <InfoCard label="Mafia" value={state.ceo.inMafia ? 'Członek' : 'Brak'} muted={!state.ceo.inMafia} />
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => doTurn()} className="bg-stone-900 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded hover:bg-stone-800 text-lg font-bold shadow-md whitespace-nowrap">
                Następna tura →
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {mainTab === 'news' && <NewsScreen />}

        {mainTab === 'management' && (
          <>
            {mgmtTab === 'changeActivity' && <ChangeActivityScreen />}
            {mgmtTab === 'party' && <PartyScreen />}
            {mgmtTab === 'competition' && <CompetitionScreen />} {/* <-- TUTAJ */}
            {mgmtTab === 'employees' && <EmployeesScreen />}
            {mgmtTab === 'office' && <OfficeScreen />}
            {mgmtTab === 'activity' && <ActivityScreen />}
            {mgmtTab === 'statistics' && <StatisticsScreen />}
            {mgmtTab === 'mafiaActivity' && <MafiaActivityScreen />}
          </>
        )}

        {mainTab === 'global' && (
          <>
            {globalTab === 'rankings' && <RankingsScreen />}
            {globalTab === 'achievements' && <AchievementsScreen />}
            {globalTab === 'stats' && <StatsScreen />}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {showOptions && <OptionsModal onClose={() => setShowOptions(false)} />}
    </div>
  );

function InfoCard({ label, value, muted }: { label: string; value: string; muted?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3">
      <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-1">{label}</div>
      <div className={`font-semibold ${muted ? 'text-stone-400' : 'text-stone-900'}`}>{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function NavButton({ active, onClick, children }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm whitespace-nowrap ${active ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-white border hover:bg-stone-50'}`}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function SubNavButton({ active, onClick, children }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} className={`px-3 py-1 text-sm whitespace-nowrap rounded-md transition ${active ? 'bg-stone-200 font-bold text-stone-900' : 'text-stone-600 hover:text-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-100'}`}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function OfficeView({ companyName, year, quarter, inMafia, inventory }: any) {
  const [view, setView] = useState<'outside' | 'inside'>('outside');

  let seasonBg = 'from-stone-200 via-stone-100 to-stone-300';
  let windowEmoji = '🌤️';
  if (quarter === 1) { seasonBg = 'from-green-200 via-green-100 to-green-300'; windowEmoji = '🌱'; }
  if (quarter === 2) { seasonBg = 'from-yellow-200 via-yellow-100 to-yellow-300'; windowEmoji = '☀️'; }
  if (quarter === 3) { seasonBg = 'from-orange-200 via-orange-100 to-orange-300'; windowEmoji = '🍂'; }
  if (quarter === 4) { seasonBg = 'from-blue-200 via-blue-100 to-blue-300'; windowEmoji = '❄️'; }

  return (
    <div className={`relative bg-gradient-to-b ${seasonBg} rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-4 h-40 md:h-64 border border-stone-200`}>
      <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 z-20">
        <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg p-1 flex text-[10px] md:text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm border border-stone-300">
          <button onClick={() => setView('outside')} className={`px-2 md:px-3 py-1 rounded-md transition ${view === 'outside' ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'text-stone-600 hover:bg-stone-200'}`}>Zewnątrz</button>
          <button onClick={() => setView('inside')} className={`px-2 md:px-3 py-1 rounded-md transition ${view === 'inside' ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'text-stone-600 hover:bg-stone-200'}`}>Wnętrze</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {view === 'outside' ? (
        <>
          <div className={`absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-3/4 bg-gradient-to-b ${seasonBg} opacity-50`} />
          <div className="absolute top-4 md:top-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 md:left-8 md:translate-x-0 w-20 md:w-28 h-14 md:h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-sky-200 to-sky-100 border-4 border-stone-400 rounded-sm shadow-inner flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">
            {windowEmoji}
            <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-0 bottom-0 w-1 bg-stone-400 -translate-x-1/2" />
            <div className="absolute top-1/2 left-0 right-0 h-1 bg-stone-400 -translate-y-1/2" />
          </div>
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-1/4 bg-amber-800/60" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-1/4 bg-gradient-to-t from-amber-900/40 to-transparent" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-10 md:bottom-16 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-48 md:w-64 h-2 bg-amber-900 rounded-sm shadow-md" />
          
          <div className="absolute top-3 md:top-4 right-3 md:right-4 text-right z-10">
            <div className="text-[9px] md:text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-stone-500 font-semibold">Siedziba firmy</div>
            <div className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-stone-900 drop-shadow-sm">{companyName}</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-stone-600 font-mono">{year} Q{quarter}</div>
            {inMafia && <div className="text-[9px] md:text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-red-700 font-bold mt-1">⚠ powiązania</div>}
          </div>

  return PARTY_LABELS[id] ?? id;

=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\App.tsx ===
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from './store/gameStore';
import { useSettingsStore } from './store/settingsStore';
import { MainMenu } from './ui/screens/MainMenu';
import { StartScreen } from './ui/screens/StartScreen';
import { OptionsScreen } from './ui/screens/OptionsScreen';
import { AboutScreen } from './ui/screens/AboutScreen';
import { Dashboard } from './ui/Dashboard';
import { SavesScreen } from './ui/screens/SavesScreen';

export default function App() {
  const screen = useGameStore(s => s.screen);
  const state = useGameStore(s => s.state);
  const theme = useSettingsStore(s => s.theme);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
  }, [theme]);

  if (screen === 'game' && state) return <Dashboard />;
  if (screen === 'start') return <StartScreen />;
  if (screen === 'options') return <OptionsScreen />;
  if (screen === 'about') return <AboutScreen />;
  if (screen === 'saves') return <SavesScreen />;
  return <MainMenu />;
}
